# RTG_RELATIONAL_RECOGNITION_DSN_v0.3.25

**Title:** RTG Relational Recognition Study — Comparators, Return Defect, and Event Strata on the Dyadic Substrate
**Date:** 27 October 2026
**Scope:** Substrate-level mathematics of *recognition* — how a relational distinction is compared with its transported self or with another distinction, what residue a closed history leaves, and which loci of the comparator/transport geometry qualify as candidate recognition events — worked on the postulated continuous dyad generator $T$ and its diagnostic shear twins. Ontological readings are carried as `[HYP]` only.
**Document class:** Technical scientific ledger (Methodology v1.4 §0.5). §1 profile exception: §1 gives RID-level readouts of the certified results with their load-bearing qualifications (about 1,300 words); a pointer-only list would drop those qualifications (Methodology v1.4 §1.1 item 3).
**Document status:** ADOPTED — PI, 27 October 2026 (four groups adopted: B1 sharpened R9 rows, A1c-E known-a sharpened schedule, A1c-D record-only outer decoder, A1c-Q record-fiber structure; stage-1 round-2 corrections reviewed, review #5664522727; no stage-2 compaction). Adoption record: `records/recog/RECOG_v0_3_25_RELEASE_RECORD.md`.
**Supersedes:** `RTG_RELATIONAL_RECOGNITION_DSN_v0.3.24` (SHA-256 `8c64bea231b3691b737f4143f6f4105030ee183fcc90d7f604ec83a4c869e7c1`; tag `recog-v0.3.24`).
**Companion documents:** current owner editions and every import pin: §7 (synchronization note) and §7.3 — Dyadic `dyad-v6.4.22`, Number Genesis `numgen-v1.20.8`, Genesis v2.8.9, OFN v0.10, RRF v0.8.4 (convention and registry reference); Discrete Genesis v2.12.13 (reference only, no import).
**DSN Methodology:** v1.4 (tag `methodology-v1.4`); `RECOG | RTG Relational Recognition` is in its namespace registry.
**Programme tier:** Tier 3 — Continuous Programme, downstream of Dyadic and Genesis (Programme Overview §6). The study prefix `RECOG` is registered in DSN Methodology v1.4 and in Programme Overview v1.2 Tier 3; the Programme entry's selected RECOG edition is updated by programme synchronization.
**Status summary:** On the explicit start set $U=\{0<Q_0\le\tfrac1{625}\}$, A1a's local extension $D_M^U$ of the Number Genesis defect formula aligns exactly with Dyadic's block phase, and $MQ_M\to1$; on the intersection with NUMGEN's existential tube $U_{\rm NUMGEN}$, $D_M^U$ equals NUMGEN's $D_M$. Hence: the limit $C_D^{\rm ext}=\lim_M(D_M^U-\tau\log M)$ exists and equals $\tau\log Q_0-M_W$ (`[THM]`, using RECOG's own R9 through Dyadic T1/T2); on $U\cap U_{\rm NUMGEN}$ it equals NUMGEN's $C_D$, and the asymptotic phase exists **without R9** (existence only, `[RESULT] [from: ALG]`, no explicit radius); quantitatively $C_D^{\rm ext}=\tau\log Q_0-\mathcal P_2(W_0)+E$ per Dyadic OPEN 21 row (`[THM]`). `[THM]` [RID: RECOG-THM-A1A-PHASE-EXISTENCE-U] (v0.3.23) On all of $U$, without R9 and without NUMGEN INF-1, $C_D^{\rm ext}$ exists and equals an explicit absolutely convergent series in the block amplitudes and the Dyadic row-A block remainders (existence and that series only). $C_D$ depends on the start state. R6 stays ACTIVE.
**Release record:** `records/recog/RECOG_v0_3_25_RELEASE_RECORD.md`. Version history: Appendix A. The earlier header — status summaries v0.2.1–v0.3.22, the full supersedes list and the freeze history — is in v0.3.22 (tag `recog-v0.3.22`).

## §1. Current Canonical State {#sec-1-state}

**Comparators.** `[DEF]` Two comparators are distinguished and must not be substituted for one another (SG-R2): the *pole comparator* $C_{\rm pole}(A,B)=e^{i\eta}$ between the two poles of one dyad, and the *state comparator* $C_\to(a,b)=\overline W_aW_b$ between two dyad states in the canonical $p=6$ eigenframe $W=\rho+\omega\eta$. `[ALG consequence]` $C_\to(a,b)=a^\top Bb-i\,a^\top BJ_Qb=I_{ab}-i\tfrac{\sqrt3}{2}\omega_{ab}$ with the certified Genesis $J_Q$ (the conjugate orientation of the Genesis $I+i\tfrac{\sqrt3}{2}\omega$ pairing). `[ALG]` [RID: RECOG-ALG-INVARIANT-FORM] Every symmetric $H$ with $A^\top HA=H$ is $H=cB$; so $C_\to$ is the canonical local comparator induced by the $p=6$, $P_1$ linear transport, up to scale and complex conjugation (SG-R9). $\widehat C$ is defined only for $Q_aQ_b>0$. `[ALG consequence]` Normalised, $\widehat C=C/\sqrt{C(a,a)C(b,b)}=e^{i\theta_{ab}}\in U(1)$, and pair swap acts as $\widehat C\mapsto\widehat C^{-1}$; swap-fixed values are exactly $\widehat C=\pm1$.

**Return defect.** `[DEF]` For a resonant return germ — $F^p$ with identity linear part at the reference fixed point — the return defect is $D_p=F^p-\mathrm{id}$; the return order $k_{\rm ret}(p)$ is the degree of its first nonzero homogeneous term modulo near-identity conjugacy. `[ALG]` [import: Dyadic §6.7 | DYAD-THM-C21] For $p=6$: $k_{\rm ret}=3$, $J_3(W)=\mu_6|W|^2W$, $\mu_6=-\tfrac12-i\tfrac{2\sqrt3}{3}$. `[ALG consequence]` $\chi_6(W)=C_\to(W,T^6W)/C_\to(W,W)=1+\mu_6Q+O(Q^{3/2})$ (raw $W$; $O(Q^2)$ post-$H_2$): the eigenvalue is the linearised recognition multiplier; $\chi$ is the diagonal case of $C$. `[ALG]` In the raw eigenframe $T^6(W)=W+\mu_6QW+O(|W|^4)$ and $\chi_6=1+\mu_6Q+O(Q^{3/2})$ (the $O(Q^2)$ remainder holds post-$H_2$, §6.2); $\chi_6(0):=1$ by continuous extension. In a small punctured neighbourhood of $P_1$, $|\chi_6|<1$, hence no nontrivial period-6 points; the recognition content of $p=6$ lives entirely in the nonlinear return germ, not in periodic-orbit spectra.

**Fixed points and swap.** `[ALG]` Zero-winding fixed points $\{P_1,P_2\}$; on the wrapped cylinder there are additional winding fixed points $(\log(1+2\pi k),0),(\log(1+2\pi k),\pi)$, $k\ge1$ (§6.3); $P_1$: $\widehat C_{\rm pole}=+1$, elliptic; $P_2$: $\widehat C_{\rm pole}=-1$, hyperbolic. `[ALG]` (v0.3.24) Inside the pole stratum $\{\widehat C_{\rm pole}=\pm1\}$ the wrapped-periodic orbits are exactly $e^\rho-1=m\pi$; odd $m$ gives period-2 orbits $(\ln(1+m\pi),0)\leftrightarrow(\ln(1+m\pi),\pi)$ with $(t_2,d_2)=(1,1-m^2\pi^2)$ (RECOG-ALG-POLE-STRATUM-PERIODIC-INVENTORY, §6.3). `[ALG conditional]` Under pole-swap covariance `[HYP]` and the certified linearisation $A$, $\rho$ must be swap-odd. `[ALG conditional]` Under the same covariance plus periodicity, $f(0)=f(\pi)=0$ and lowest-harmonic minimality, the radial forcing is $f(\eta)\propto\sin\eta$.

**Shear decomposition and jet law.** `[ALG]` $T=T_s+T_b$ with $T_s=(\rho-\sin\eta,\ \eta+\sinh\rho-\sin\eta)$ the exact swap-odd projection and $T_b=(0,\cosh\rho-1)$ the entire non-equivariant content; $T_b$ is the only quadratic term of the primitive jet. `[ALG]` [RID: RECOG-ALG-SHEAR-JET] For $g(\rho)=\rho+a\rho^2+b\rho^3+O(\rho^4)$, radial row $\rho'=\rho-\sin\eta$:
$$\boxed{\mu_6(a,b)=3b-4a^2-i\sqrt3\left(\tfrac13+2a^2-b\right)}.$$
Healing $\iff 4a^2>3b$. `[ALG]` [RID: RECOG-ALG-CUBIC-RETURN-RIGIDITY] $|\mu_6(a,b)|^2=12\bigl(b-\tfrac32a^2-\tfrac1{12}\bigr)^2+\tfrac14(2a^2+1)^2\ge\tfrac14$, hence $\mu_6(a,b)\ne0$ and $k_{\rm ret}(6)=3$ for every real $(a,b)$. `[ALG diagnostic]` $\mu_6^{(s)}=\tfrac12-i\tfrac{\sqrt3}{6}$ (expansive), $\mu_6^{(\log)}=-i\tfrac{\sqrt3}{3}$ (radially neutral), $\mu_6^{(\tanh)}=-1-i\tfrac{2\sqrt3}{3}$ (healing, swap-symmetric). `[ALG consequence]` On the radial-fate boundary $4a^2=3b$: $\mu_6=-i\tfrac{\sqrt3}{3}(1+2a^2)$, purely angular and nonzero; at the rigidity minimiser $\mu_6(0,\tfrac1{12})=\tfrac12e^{-i\pi/3}$. `[RESULT]` `[from: ALG]` Within the shear-jet family the return-defect order is rigid — no member delays the first return defect beyond cubic order — while the return content ($\Re\mu$, $\arg\mu$) and hence the radial fate (healing / neutral / expansion) are shear-selected; the fate can change class with $k_{\rm ret}$ unchanged and an angular residue surviving. Healing does **not** require swap-symmetry breaking.

**Candidate strata.** `[DEF]` Three candidate mathematical strata with a prerequisite ladder: rank stratum ($\det DF_x=0$; one state), pair-symmetry stratum ($\widehat C=\pm1$; pole level: primitive co-presence of $A,B$; state level: co-present states or retained self-comparison), loop/spectral stratum ($(\operatorname{tr}M_n,\det M_n)$; closed transport). `[ALG]` (v0.3.24) The pole-comparator quadrature $\{\operatorname{Re}\widehat C_{\rm pole}=0\}$ is the locus of extremal one-step radial transfer, $|\rho(Tx)-\rho(x)|=1$; its primitive-chart unipotent type has no chart-free content (RECOG-ALG-QUADRATURE-TYPING, §6.5). `[ALG]` Of single-step Jacobian invariants, rank and the sign of $\det DF_x$ are pointwise chart-free on a connected chart; eigenvalue type is not. `[HYP]` [RID: RECOG-OPEN-INTERSECTION] (registered under OPEN R6; the word OPEN in the RID is identity text, not status) Recognition event $=$ nontrivial algebraic stratum $\cap$ dynamically distinguished stratum — realised by $P_2$, but in the dyad this coincidence is built in, not discovered (§6.5).

**Resonant-return family ($p\ge5$).** `[ALG]` [RID: RECOG-ALG-LEADING-RETURN-EQUIVARIANCE] If $F^p=\mathrm{id}+G_k+\cdots$ is tangent to identity with $L^p=I$, then $LG_k=G_k\circ L$ (from $F\circ F^p=F^p\circ F$; authoritative statement and proof §6.12). `[ALG]` [scope: resonant normal-form frame] [RID: RECOG-ALG-COMPARATOR-CLOSURE] Charge selection $\#W-\#\bar W\equiv1\pmod p$ forces literal charge $1$ through degree $p-2$; the return content is then a polynomial in $Q_a$ and $C_\to(a,b)$ for any number of modes; degree $p-1$ admits the first non-comparator channel $\bar W^{p-1}$. `[ALG]` [import: DYAD-ALG-BRIDGE-LEMMA, general form] For $p\ge5$: $[F_p^p-\mathrm{id}]_2=0$, $[F_p^p-\mathrm{id}]_3=p\bar\lambda\nu_3^{(1,p)}Z^2\bar Z$. `[ALG]` [RID: RECOG-ALG-NU3-CLOSED-FORM] $\nu_3(\lambda)$ as in the status summary; verified exactly mod $\Phi_5,\Phi_7$, by raw composition for $p=5\ldots12$, and $6\bar\lambda\nu_3(\zeta_6)=3\mu_6^{W}$ (the $p=6$ case of the frame scale DYAD-ALG-KP-FRAME-SCALE; $\nu_3$ and $\mu_p$ here are $Z$-frame quantities). `[ALG]` [RID: RECOG-ALG-NU3-NONVANISHING] numerator $N$ has $\gcd(N,\lambda^5N(1/\lambda))=1$ and $\gcd(N,\Phi_p)=1$ for all $\varphi(p)\le5$, hence $k_{\rm ret}(p)=3$ for every $p\ge5$. `[ALG]` (named sub-statement of RECOG-ALG-NU3-CLOSED-FORM, $Z$-frame) $\Re(\bar\lambda\nu_3(e^{i\theta}))=-\sin^2(\theta/2)$ exactly, hence $\Re\mu_p=-p\sin^2(\pi/p)<0$ for all $p$ in the $Z$-frame: healing for every $p$, $\Re\mu_p\sim-\pi^2/p$, $\arg\mu_p$ monotone toward $-90^\circ$ ($-120.96^\circ,-113.41^\circ,-108.33^\circ$ at $p=5,6,7$; `[OBS finite-p]`).

**Lattice readability, clock, memory ($p=6$).** `[ALG]` [frame: post-$H_2$ six-step normal form] $\chi_6=1+\mu Q+(c_{32}+c_{05}e^{-6i\Theta})Q^2+\cdots$ with $c_{32}=-\tfrac{97}{432}+i\tfrac{197\sqrt3}{324}$, $c_{05}=-\tfrac{49}{8640}-i\tfrac{307\sqrt3}{25920}$ (derived from the primitive map with the Dyadic $h_2$; §6.13). `[ALG]` [RID: RECOG-ALG-LATTICE-STRENGTH] $\mathcal I_{\rm lat}=|c_{05}|/|\mu|^2\approx0.013443$ (linear-frame and $U(1)$-equivariant-residual-gauge invariant). `[ALG consequence]` [RID: RECOG-ALG-LATTICE-CLOCK] $\Psi_{\rm lat}=\arg c_{05}-6\Theta$ is invariant under linear frame changes (not under nonlinear equivariant gauge, SG-R10); successive lattice thresholds along a healing orbit satisfy $m_{k+1}/m_k\to e^{\pi/(3\tau)}=\lambda_{\rm DSI}^{1/6}=(R^*)^{1/3}\approx2.4766$ (rate imported from NUMGEN defect-threshold theorem); per-threshold modulation fades as $\lambda_{\rm DSI}^{-1/3}$. `[ALG consequence, leading order]` [RID: RECOG-ALG-LATTICE-MEMORY] $\Lambda(Q_0,\Theta_0)=\tfrac{Q_0}{1270080}[43\sin6\Theta_0-5611\sqrt3\cos6\Theta_0]+O(Q_0^2)$ (post-$H_2$ frame); no retained $\log Q$ memory. `[THM]` In the delivered exact $\widehat F$, $M=q_0[c_{\rm iso}+\Lambda_1(\Theta_0)]+O(q_0^2)$ with explicit row-dependent bounds; on the $C_z$ track, identical comparator data can retain different asymptotic pair phases for $q_0<|G|/C_2$ (RECOG-THM-R9-COMPARATOR-INCOMPLETENESS, §6.13a); sharpened rows (v0.3.25) $C_2=259.2$ / $81.49$ from the summation lemma RECOG-ALG-R9-SUM-LEMMA, and $221.4$ / $75.69$ `[assuming: Dyadic $C_7$ certificate ceilings]` (RECOG-THM-R9-SHARPENED-ROWS, -C7; the adopted rows stay in force).

## §3. Scope Guards {#sec-3-guards}

**[SG-R1]** No result in this document is an ontological theorem. Substrate results may be *cited* in the ontology note as realisation witnesses; they may not be *promoted* there. The ontology is prior to RTG, not derived from it.
Violation: category error; the ontological claim is `[HYP]` at best.

**[SG-R2]** The pole comparator $C_{\rm pole}=e^{i\eta}$ and the state comparator $C_\to(a,b)=\overline W_aW_b$ are distinct objects at distinct levels. Neither may be substituted for the other; $\arg W\ne\eta$ ($\arg W(P_2)=2\pi/3$). Any "antipode" statement must name its comparator.
Violation: mis-assigned event angle; CELL-B output becomes meaningless.

**[SG-R3]** The eigenvalue type (elliptic/parabolic/hyperbolic) of a single-step Jacobian $DF_x$ off a fixed point is chart-dependent ($DF_x\mapsto D\varphi_{F(x)}DF_xD\varphi_x^{-1}$). Pointwise chart-free under this conjugacy on a connected chart: the rank and the **sign of $\det DF_x$** (orientation character), since $\det D\varphi$ has constant sign; the rank-loss locus $C_0$ is the boundary between orientation-preserving and orientation-reversing transport. Type statements are intrinsic only for closed transports $M_n$ on periodic points.
Violation: the quadrature locus $\cos\eta=0$ is `[ALG]` [scope: primitive chart] only (its chart-free content is comparator-geometric, RECOG-ALG-QUADRATURE-TYPING, §6.5).

**[SG-R4]** Shear twins $T_s$, $T_{\log}$, $T_{\tanh}$ and the $(a,b)$-family are diagnostic objects. They are not generator candidates and export nothing to Genesis, Triadic or TDR-G.
Violation: contaminates OPEN-P0 with unmotivated alternatives.

**[SG-R5]** No continuous↔discrete transfer. The TDR-G antipodal defect band $\mathcal D_k$ and the continuous $P_2$ geometry are related only as *two witnesses*; STAR-NOERASE-1 and the $Q_c$ protection mechanism are witnesses in different substrates. A bridge theorem is required before "same mechanism" claims.
Violation: SG-14 of the Dyadic DSN.

**[SG-R6]** Nontriviality of a comparison ($\widehat C\ne1$, or $D_\gamma\ne0$) is not a recognition *event*. Comparison, event, and memory are three distinct layers (§6.8). "Recognised" is used only for the mathematical residue; "event" is reserved for OPEN R6.
Violation: tautology ("everything compares, hence everything is recognised").

**[SG-R7]** All results inherit `[scope: conditional on postulated continuous generator T]` from the Dyadic export protocol. Jet-law statements are cubic-order, small-$Q$ statements.
Violation: overreach beyond the local germ.

**[SG-R8]** $Q<Q_c$ certifies only that the rank stratum $C_0$ is unreachable *at the crossing step*. It is a sufficient local safety condition, not a no-erasure theorem and not a global statement.
Violation: treating a local barrier as a global protection law.

**[SG-R9]** The metric $B$ is intrinsic up to scale; the complex orientation of the comparator is fixed only after the Genesis $J_Q$ sign branch is chosen. $C_\to=\overline W_aW_b$ carries the conjugate orientation of the Genesis $I+i\tfrac{\sqrt3}{2}\omega$ pairing. Convention-safe quantities: $|C|$, amplitude ratios, the swap-fixed set $\widehat C=\pm1$. Sign-carrying quantities ($\arg\widehat C$, $\Im\mu$) must state the orientation.
Violation: sign errors in phase-defect and memory statements.

**[SG-R10]** Lattice quantities carry three scopes. $\mathcal I_{\rm lat}$, the clock ratio and the leading $\Lambda$ are invariant under linear frame changes and $U(1)$-equivariant residual gauge; $\Psi_{\rm lat}$ is invariant under linear frame changes only (under $\widetilde W=e^{ia|W|^2}W$ it shifts by $-6aQ$); $\arg c_{05}$, $|c_{05}|/|\beta|$ and individual crossing indices are frame-convention quantities. **All quintic coefficients must name their frame** (raw six-step vs post-$H_2$ vs final gauge); v0.3 numbers quoted without a frame are superseded (Appendix B). $\Lambda$ is leading order in $Q_0$ only; the quoted leading amplitude (currently $0.007652\,Q_0$, post-$H_2$) is not a finite-$Q_0$ bound for the full exact memory. The certified finite-$q_0$ statement is RECOG-THM-R9-MEMORY (§6.13a): per row, on the $C_z$ track, in the normalized $q$, $|M-c_{\rm iso}q_0|\le|G|q_0+C_2q_0^2$, hence $|M|\le(|c_{\rm iso}|+|G|)q_0+C_2q_0^2$; comparator incompleteness is certified only for $q_0<|G|/C_2$ (RECOG-THM-R9-COMPARATOR-INCOMPLETENESS). Lattice thresholds are a mathematical sequence; "event" is `[HYP]`.
Violation: convention-laden numbers presented as invariants; asymptotic coefficients presented as bounds.

**[SG-R11]** Two rigidity theorems, two families. RECOG-ALG-CUBIC-RETURN-RIGIDITY is over shear jets at $p=6$, $K=1$; RECOG-ALG-NU3-NONVANISHING is over $p$ at fixed primitive shear in the $K_p$ family. They coincide at $p=6$ and must not be merged.
Violation: invoking $|\mu_6(a,b)|\ge\tfrac12$ for $p\ne6$, or the all-$p$ result for modified shears.

**[SG-R12]** The linear eigenframe $W=\rho+\omega\eta$ does not descend to the phase cylinder ($W(\rho,\eta+2\pi)=W+2\pi\omega$). $Q$, $C_\to$ and state-comparator phases are defined on a lifted chart, or with the Dyadic wrap $\eta_{\rm wrap}\in(-\pi,\pi]$ and its seam convention; $W(P_2)$ uses the representative $+\pi$ ($\arg W=2\pi/3$; $-\pi$ gives the negative). $|C_\to|$ is not invariant under $B\to cB$; $\widehat C$ and amplitude ratios are.
Violation: comparing state comparators across the seam or across lifts.

**[SG-R13]** (v0.3.2, adopts Dyadic SG-19) Three operations are all historically called "normalization": the archival coordinate change $\Phi_{\rm archive}$, the log-channel projection $G_{\rm proj}$, and the polynomial conjugacy $\mathcal J_{\le6}(f\circ h-h\circ n)=0$ with additive $h$. RECOG uses only the third; every "normalized" coefficient names its operation; no coefficient is transferred between operations. Grammar: $\widehat f_{\rm form}$ (formal normal form), $\widehat f_6$/$J$ (finite jets), $\widehat F$ (delivered exact map on its proved domain) are distinct symbols; a symmetry of $J$ or of the formal normal form is never stated as a symmetry of $\widehat F$; conjugacy identities carry the $\mathcal J_{\le6}$ qualifier.
Violation: inserting raw $c_{05}$, $c_\theta^{(6)}$, $a_1$, $a_2$ or a long-orbit backbone into the chart; writing $f\circ h=h\circ n$ without $\mathcal J_{\le6}$; calling $-z$ an exact symmetry of $\widehat F$.

**[SG-R14]** (v0.3.2) Comparator typing. Every comparator statement names its track: $C_{\rm pole}=e^{i\eta}$ (pole level); $C_W=\overline W_aW_b$ (canonical Genesis, raw $\mathcal L$-frame); $C_z=\bar z_az_b$ (NF-native); $C_W^{(h)}=\overline{h(z_a)}h(z_b)$ (canonical transported to the chart); $C^W_{\rm cov}=(h^{-1})^*C_z$ (NF-native transported to $W$). Covariance belongs to the pair (observable, action), not to a chart: $C_z$ is exactly $(-I)$-covariant, $C_W$ is $S_h$-covariant only to $O(2)$. Two $z$-states may be compared by $C_z$ only as preimages under the same registered $h$.
Violation: an untyped "the comparator"; treating $S_h$-covariance of $C_W$ as exact; identifying $q$ with $Q_{\rm prim}$.
*Raw-track scope (v0.3.4).* Identical $C_W^{(h)}$ data **need not** give identical $C_z$ data: $h_2$ is not $U(1)$-equivariant, so the induced $C_z$-track mismatch is **generically** $O(\sqrt{q_0})$ (special configurations may cancel it). Raw-comparator equality therefore does not control the $C_z$ datum to the $O(q_0)$ precision needed to isolate lattice memory; memory-incompleteness statements are made in the $C_z$ track. Because $h(z)=z+O(z^2)$, the chart correction to phase vanishes as $z\to0$, so the *limiting* pair phase agrees between the two tracks — final observables are track-independent, finite-initial-state comparator data are not.
*Quantified.* [import: RTG_DYADIC_OSCILLATOR_DSN_v6.4.22 §6.14 (producer edition v6.4.15) | DYAD-THM-OPEN3-ASYMPTOTIC-PHASE, T2 `[THM]` `[assuming: T1]`; T1 `[THM]` `[assuming: RECOG-THM-R9-MEMORY]`] On the primitive asymptotic-phase track, $M_W-M=-\Delta_0(z_0)$ with $|M_W-M|\le K_0\sqrt{q_0}$; $K_0=\tfrac{1101}{1000}$ on $|z|\le\tfrac1{20}$ and $K_0=\tfrac{533}{500}$ on $|z|\le\tfrac1{40}$, rows not mixed (DYAD-THM-OPEN3-PHASE-OFFSET-BOUNDS). $M_W$ is the primitive-phase memory constructed through Dyadic's transported asymptotic-phase observable; this is not a theorem about the comparator $C_W^{(h)}$, and no R9 coefficient is transferred to that track. T1 and T2 are in adopted use in §6.13b (RECOG-THM-A1A-CD-IDENTIFICATION, R9 premise discharged by RECOG's own R9); this note adds no claim.

## §6. Main Scientific Ledger {#sec-6-ledger}

### §6.1 Comparators `[ALG consequence / mixed]` {#sec-6-1-comparators}

> **[Local guard SG-R2]** Pole and state comparators are distinct; name the comparator in every antipode statement.

`[DEF]` [RID: RECOG-DEF-POLE-COMPARATOR] **Pole comparator.** For a single dyad with relative phase $\eta$, $C_{\rm pole}(A,B)=e^{i\eta}$. Pole swap $A\leftrightarrow B$ sends $\eta\mapsto-\eta$, hence $C_{\rm pole}\mapsto C_{\rm pole}^{-1}$.

`[DEF]` [RID: RECOG-DEF-STATE-COMPARATOR] **State comparator.** In the canonical eigenframe $W=\rho+\omega\eta$, $\omega=\tfrac12(-1+i\sqrt3)$ (Dyadic §6.2), for two dyad states $a,b$:
$$C_\to(a,b):=\overline W_aW_b.$$
The arrow marks the orientation: $C_\to(a,b)$ compares $a$ *to* $b$, so that the diagonal specialisation $b=F(a)$ gives $W'/W$ (§6.2).

`[ALG consequence]` [import: Genesis §6.1 ($B$, $Q=r^\top Br$, $A^\top BA=B$), Genesis §6.6 ($I_{ab}$, $\omega_{ab}$, $J_Q=\tfrac{1}{\sqrt3}\begin{psmallmatrix}1&-2\\2&-1\end{psmallmatrix}$, $a^\top BJ_Qb=+\tfrac{\sqrt3}{2}\omega_{ab}$)] [cert: `recog_shear_jet_cert.py` checks (10)] Since $|W|^2=\rho^2-\rho\eta+\eta^2=r^\top Br$ exactly, polarisation gives
$$C_\to(a,b)=a^\top Bb-i\,a^\top BJ_Qb=I_{ab}-i\tfrac{\sqrt3}{2}\omega_{ab},\qquad W_a\overline W_b=I_{ab}+i\tfrac{\sqrt3}{2}\omega_{ab}.$$
$C_\to$ is the conjugate orientation of the Genesis $+$ pairing. `[COR]` v0.1 stated $\overline W_aW_b=I_{ab}+i\tfrac{\sqrt3}{2}\omega_{ab}$; the sign was wrong. See Appendix B, RID: RECOG-DEF-COMPARATOR-SIGN-V01.

`[ALG]` [RID: RECOG-ALG-INVARIANT-FORM] [cert: check (11)] **Invariant-form uniqueness.** With $A=\begin{psmallmatrix}1&-1\\1&0\end{psmallmatrix}$, the linear system $A^\top HA=H$ on symmetric $2\times2$ matrices has the one-dimensional solution space $H=cB$; positive definiteness gives $c>0$. The scale cancels in every ratio below. `[RESULT]` `[from: ALG]` $C_\to$ is the canonical local comparator induced by the $p=6$, $P_1$ linear transport $A$ — up to scale, and up to complex conjugation until the $J_Q$ sign branch is fixed (SG-R9). No global transport-invariance of $C_\to$ over the nonlinear state space is claimed.

`[ALG consequence]` **Normalised comparator and swap.**
$$\widehat C(a,b)=\frac{C_\to(a,b)}{\sqrt{C_\to(a,a)C_\to(b,b)}}=e^{i\theta_{ab}}\in U(1),\qquad \widehat C(b,a)=\overline{\widehat C(a,b)}=\widehat C(a,b)^{-1}.$$
Here $\theta_{ab}=\arg W_b-\arg W_a$ in the $C_\to$ orientation (SG-R9). [scope: $\widehat C$ is defined only for $Q_aQ_b>0$; $C_\to$ itself is defined at the zero-distinction state, $\widehat C$ is not.]
Swap-fixed values: $z=z^{-1}\iff z=\pm1$. $+1$ is the identity comparison; $-1$ is the unique nonidentity swap-fixed comparison. This characterisation is purely algebraic and needs no metric notion of "maximal distinction" (which remains an OFN interpretation).

`[DEF]` **Chart.** $W$ is taken on the lifted $(\rho,\eta)$ plane or on the Dyadic wrap $\eta_{\rm wrap}\in(-\pi,\pi]$ (SG-R12); all comparator values below are chart-relative in this sense, and $|C_\to|$ carries the scale of $B$.

`[ALG consequence]` **Two-coordinate comparison.** $C_\to(a,b)/C_\to(a,a)=\sqrt{Q_b/Q_a}\,e^{i\theta_{ab}}$; the natural comparison output is the dimensionless pair $(Q_b/Q_a,\ \Delta\theta_{ab})$.

`[HEUR]` The ratio appears here as the output of the comparison form, not as a primitive. This is *not* evidence that ratio precedes difference (Number Genesis guard); it is evidence that closed self-comparison is dimensionless.

### §6.2 Return Defect and the Six-Step Multiplier `[ALG / mixed]` {#sec-6-2-return}

`[DEF]` **Resonant return germ, return defect, return order.** Let $F$ have a fixed point $x_*$ at which $DF^p(x_*)=I$ for some $p$ (a resonant return germ). Define $D_p:=F^p-\mathrm{id}$; its first nonzero homogeneous term (modulo near-identity conjugacy) is the return defect and its degree is $k_{\rm ret}(p)$, with $k_{\rm ret}=\infty$ if $D_p$ vanishes to all orders. $D_n=F^n-\mathrm{id}$ is definable for every $n$; what the resonance condition supplies is a tangent-to-identity return, so that the leading term of $D_p$ is a formal invariant. `[COR]` v0.1 defined $D_\gamma$ over an "exact closed history"; the central object $T^6$ is not a nonlinear closed history off $P_1$. See Appendix B, RID: RECOG-DEF-RETURN-DEFECT-V01. The leading term $J_{k_{\rm ret}}$ is the formal invariant of a tangent-to-identity map under near-identity changes; its magnitude is convention-dependent under linear rescaling $W\mapsto cW$ (the vanishing/non-vanishing and, at fixed orientation convention, $\arg\mu$ and the radial/angular ratio are robust).

`[ALG]` [import: Dyadic §6.7 | DYAD-THM-C21] For $p=6$ the six-step quadratic part vanishes identically (master parity lemma), the cubic residual collapses to the resonant $(2,1)$ channel, and
$$T^6(W)=W+\mu_6|W|^2W+O(|W|^4)\ \ (\text{raw frame}),\qquad \mu_6=-\tfrac12-i\tfrac{2\sqrt3}{3},\qquad k_{\rm ret}(6)=3;$$
the $O(|W|^5)$ form holds only for the odd projection or in the normal-form chart, which are different objects (Dyadic SG-19, §6.12; next paragraph).

`[ALG]` **Remainder order by frame** [`[COR]` after external audit]. In the raw eigenframe the quartic six-step jet is nonzero (channels $(4,0),(3,1),(2,2),(1,3)$; on $(\rho,\eta)=(t,0)$: $W(T^6(t,0))=t+\mu_6t^3-\tfrac{5+25i\sqrt3}{48}t^4+O(t^5)$), so $T^6(W)=W+\mu_6QW+O(|W|^4)$ and $\chi_6=1+\mu_6Q+O(Q^{3/2})$, $|\chi_6|=1-\tfrac12Q+O(Q^{3/2})$, $\arg\chi_6=-\tau Q+O(Q^{3/2})$. The $O(|W|^5)$ / $O(Q^2)$ remainder holds in the post-$H_2$ frame, which is *not* the linear $W$ of the exact comparator identity. Within the tangent-to-identity frame changes compared here, the leading coefficient, return order and local strict contraction are frame-independent; under a complex linear rescaling the return order, local strict contraction, the sign of $\Re\mu$ and $\arg\mu$ are kept and $|\mu|$ scales (frame rule, DYAD-ALG-KP-FRAME-SCALE).

`[ALG consequence]` **Recognition multiplier.** With $b=T^6a$ in §6.1,
$$\chi_6(W):=\frac{C_\to(W,T^6W)}{C_\to(W,W)}=\frac{W'}{W}=\sqrt{\tfrac{Q'}{Q}}\,e^{i\Delta\Theta}=1+\mu_6Q+O(Q^{3/2})\quad(\text{raw }W;\ O(Q^2)\text{ in the post-}H_2\text{ coordinate}),$$
consistent with the certified leading laws $Q'=Q-Q^2+\cdots$, $\Delta\Theta=-\tau Q+\cdots$ (Dyadic §6.8) — leading terms only: in the raw frame the next amplitude term is phase-dependent and of order $Q^{5/2}$ (on the ray $(t,0)$: $-\tfrac5{24}Q^{5/2}$), and the typed chart laws with their remainders are in §6.14; no autonomous scalar closure is implied. $\arg W=\Theta$ (ISP) exactly. The one-step eigenvalue $\lambda$ is the linearised special case $R(v)=\lambda v\Rightarrow R(v)/v=\lambda$; at the recognition cycle $\lambda^6=1$ and the linear spectrum carries no information — the content is in $J_3$.

`[ALG]` **Local exact statements.** $\chi_6$ is defined for $W\ne0$; $\lim_{W\to0}\chi_6=1$, so $\chi_6(0):=1$ is a continuous extension (the map gives $D_6(0)=0$ directly). For $0<Q<\epsilon$: $|\chi_6|=1-\tfrac12Q+O(Q^{3/2})<1$ (raw $W$), hence $\chi_6\ne1$ and $T^6$ has no fixed point other than $P_1$ in the punctured sector. Depends on: [import: RTG_NUMBER_GENESIS_DSN_v1.15 | NUMGEN-RESULT-L1-PRIMITIVE-CONTRACTION] $Q_{\rm true}(Fx)-Q_{\rm true}(x)=-Q_{\rm true}^2+O(Q_{\rm true}^{5/2})$ with phase-uniform strict contraction on a sufficiently small punctured sector. See §7. Substrate statement: $Q=0\Rightarrow D_6=0$; $0<Q<\epsilon\Rightarrow T^6x\ne x$ with nontrivial return defect. Any reading of this as a witness for an ontological thesis is made in the ontology note, not here (§6.9).

`[ALG]` **Distinction anchoring.** $\chi_6$ compares $W$ with $T^6W$ relative to $W=0$, i.e. relative to the zero-distinction state $\mathcal O_0=0$ of Genesis §6.1 — not relative to an external origin. The definition is *distinction-anchored*, not origin-free; a genuine torsor difference on the state space would use only the phase/winding gauge, which is invisible to the dynamics (Dyadic §6.4) and hence empty.

`[ALG]` **Phase readability.** Phase structure exists at linear order ($\lambda=e^{i\pi/3}$); a *self-readable* phase defect first appears at cubic order, multiplied by amplitude: $\arg\chi_6=-\tau Q+O(Q^{3/2})$ (raw $W$; $O(Q^2)$ post-$H_2$). Phase is not created by $Q$; its self-recognition residue opens with $Q$.

$$T_s(x)=\tfrac12\bigl(T(x)-T(-x)\bigr)=(\rho-\sin\eta,\ \eta+\sinh\rho-\sin\eta),\qquad T_b(x)=\tfrac12\bigl(T(x)+T(-x)\bigr)=(0,\ \cosh\rho-1),$$
$T=T_s+T_b$. Equivalently $T\circ S_o-S_o\circ T=(0,\,2(\cosh\rho-1))=(0,\rho^2+O(\rho^4))$: $T$ is swap-equivariant at linear order and breaks swap symmetry first at quadratic order. $T_b$ is the *only* quadratic term of the primitive one-step jet; consequently the certified quadratic normal-form coefficients $c_{20},c_{11},c_{02}$ (Dyadic §6.2) are entirely sourced by the symmetry-breaking term.

### §6.6a CELL-G1 — Ratio-Composition Laws for the Phase Shear (R5) `[ALG]` {#sec-6-6a-composition}

> **Scope guard (SG-R4, OPEN-P0).** A classification of composition laws. The functions $g_k$ below (including $\log r$ and $\sinh\rho$) appear as diagnostic classification objects, **not** as generator candidates; nothing is exported to Genesis, Triadic or TDR-G. No claim that any composition premise holds physically; no restoration of pole-swap symmetry of $T$ (the decomposition $T=T_s+T_b$ and its even quadratic term stand).

`[DEF]` Write the primitive phase drive as $g(r)$, $r=e^\rho>0$ the relational ratio (primitive: $g(r)=r-1=e^\rho-1$; exact wrapped form $\nu'=\nu\,e^{i[m-1-s(\nu)]}$, $m=e^\rho$). Fix the **power character** $\chi_k(r)=r^k$, $k\in\mathbb R$. A **$\chi_k$-twisted cocycle law** is
$$g(rs)=g(r)+\chi_k(r)\,g(s)\qquad(r,s>0),$$
i.e. the drive composes additively along relational composition $r_{AC}=r_{AB}r_{BC}$ after transporting the second increment by the action $\chi_k$. Normalisation $g'(1)=1$. Normalised notation: $g_k(r)=\dfrac{r^k-1}{k}$ for $k\ne0$, $g_0(r)=\log r$ (the $k\to0$ limit).

`[ALG]` [RID: RECOG-ALG-SHEAR-COCYCLE-CLASSIFICATION] (a) Every solution has $g(1)=0$ (set $r=s=1$). (b) **$k\ne0$:** commutativity gives $(1-\chi_k(s))g(r)=(1-\chi_k(r))g(s)$, so $g(r)/(r^k-1)$ is constant on $r\ne1$ and $g=c\,(r^k-1)$, **with no regularity assumption on $g$**; each such $g$ is a coboundary, $g(r)=\chi_k(r)c-c$ (the displacement of a fixed reference under the action). With $g'(1)=1$, $c=1/k$, i.e. $g=g_k$; for $k=1$, $g(r)=r-1$ **uniquely**. (c) **$k=0$:** the Cauchy equation $g(rs)=g(r)+g(s)$; under measurability (or continuity at a point) $g=c\log r$, and $g'(1)=1$ gives $g_0=\log r=\rho$; here $g$ is a genuine class, not a coboundary.

`[ALG]` [RID: RECOG-ALG-SHEAR-INVERSION-PROJECTION] Under inversion $r\mapsto r^{-1}$ ($\rho\mapsto-\rho$ in the lift), $g_k(r^{-1})=-g_{-k}(r)$: inversion maps the weight-$k$ law to weight $-k$. The inversion odd and even projections of the primitive cocycle $g_1$ are
$$\tfrac12\big[g_1(r)-g_1(r^{-1})\big]=\tfrac12(r-r^{-1})=\sinh\rho,\qquad \tfrac12\big[g_1(r)+g_1(r^{-1})\big]=\tfrac12(r+r^{-1}-2)=\cosh\rho-1,$$
equivalently $\sinh\rho=\tfrac12(g_1+g_{-1})$ and $\cosh\rho-1=\tfrac12(g_1-g_{-1})$. Hence $e^\rho-1=\sinh\rho+(\cosh\rho-1)$ (§6.6) is exactly the inversion odd/even split of the primitive cocycle, and $T=T_s+T_b$ is its dynamical form. $\sinh\rho$ mixes the characters $k=\pm1$ and is a twisted cocycle for **no** single power character.

`[ALG]` [RID: RECOG-ALG-SHEAR-ORIENTATION-CONJUGACY] With $T_k(\rho,\eta)=(\rho-\sin\eta,\ \eta+g_k(e^\rho)-\sin\eta)$ and the pole swap $S(\rho,\eta)=(-\rho,-\eta)$ (the point reflection of the swap parity, §6.6), the identity $-g_k(e^{-\rho})=g_{-k}(e^\rho)$ gives
$$S\circ T_k\circ S=T_{-k}\qquad\text{exactly, for every }k\in\mathbb R\ (\text{including }k=0).$$
Consequences: (i) **no conjugacy-invariant observable of the isolated dyad dynamics distinguishes $k$ from $-k$** — periodic-orbit multipliers and spectra, return-defect invariants, appropriately typed normal-form invariants (for the $p=6$ resonant normal form the resonant monomials have $m-n\equiv1\pmod6$, so $m+n$ is odd and conjugation by $z\mapsto-z$ leaves their coefficients unchanged), and any dynamical statement transported covariantly by $S$; $\mu_6(k)=\mu_6(-k)$ (below) is the cubic member of this exact equivalence; (ii) $k=0$ is self-conjugate, $S\circ T_0\circ S=T_0$: within the character family the swap-symmetric member is exactly the trivial-character one. **Scope:** conjugacy invariants agree and covariantly defined structures correspond; statements about a particular coordinate-side locus are not literally identical but are carried to the corresponding $S$-transported locus.

$$\mu_6(k)=-\frac{k^2}{2}-i\frac{\sqrt3}{3}(1+k^2)$$
(and $\mu_6(0)=-i\sqrt3/3$, the log row). Consequences: (i) $\mu_6(k)=\mu_6(-k)$ — the cubic return residue is **blind to orientation**; (ii) within the character family, $\mu_6(k)=\mu_6^{\rm prim}=-\tfrac12-i\tfrac{2\sqrt3}3$ iff $k^2=1$, so **$|k|=1$ is identifiable** from the certified primitive cubic return content while the sign is not. This is an inverse identification from the given map's return data, not a foundational derivation of the weight.

(higher-arity structure; routed to RRF, identification before network carrier, OPEN-B → OPEN-E1, and OFN/OPEN-P0); **(P2) weight magnitude** — why $|k|=1$ rather than $k=0$ or another $|k|\ne1$? ### §6.12 Leading-Return Equivariance and Comparator Closure `[ALG / mixed]` {#sec-6-12-equivariance}

`[ALG]` [RID: RECOG-ALG-LEADING-RETURN-EQUIVARIANCE] **Theorem.** Let $F=L+F_2+\cdots$ with $L^p=I$ and $F^p=\mathrm{id}+G_k+O(k+1)$, $G_k\ne0$. Then $LG_k(x)=G_k(Lx)$.
*Proof.* Expand $F\circ F^p=F^p\circ F$ at degree $k$: the left side gives $LG_k(x)+F_k(x)$, the right side $F_k(x)+G_k(Lx)$ (all other contributions have degree $\ge k+1$). $\square$ Gauge-independent; valid for any number of modes with $L=\operatorname{diag}(A,\ldots,A)$.

`[ALG]` [scope: resonant normal-form frame] [RID: RECOG-ALG-COMPARATOR-CLOSURE] In the eigenframe, $\mathbb Z_p$-equivariance of an output-$W_a$ monomial requires $\#W-\#\bar W\equiv1\pmod p$. For total degree $\le p-2$ this forces $\#W-\#\bar W=1$, i.e. $U(1)$-equivariance, so every such term is $\sum_bW_bP_{ab}(\{C_\to(c,d)\})$ (cubic: $\sum\alpha W_bC_\to(c,d)$): the return content lies in the comparator algebra $\{Q_a,C_\to(a,b)\}$. At degree $p-1$ the monomial $\bar W^{p-1}$ ($\#W-\#\bar W=-(p-1)\equiv1$) is the first symmetry-allowed non-comparator channel. Genesis' certified $\Delta Q_d^{(4)}$, written entirely in $Q,I_{ab},\omega_{ab}$, is an instance.

`[ALG consequence]` **Uncoupled pair.** For two states of one map, $\Delta\log C_\to=\bar\mu Q_a+\mu Q_b+O(Q^{3/2})$ for the raw comparator (nonzero raw quartic jet; $O(Q^2)$ holds for $C_z$ in the NF chart), so $\Delta\theta_{ab}=-\tau(Q_b-Q_a)$ at leading order: the encounter residue is the difference of the two self-return residues; equal amplitudes build no relative phase at cubic order; integrating along $Q_k(t)=q_k/(1+q_kt)$ reproduces $\Phi_{ab}(\infty)=\Phi_{ab}(0)-\tau\log(q_a/q_b)$ (Genesis §6.6) as the **leading plateau surrogate**; the exact-map retained phase adds $M(z_{b,0})-M(z_{a,0})$ (RECOG-THM-R9-MEMORY, §1). Genuine encounter content is the off-diagonal $\alpha$ of coupled systems (Genesis torque channels).

`[RESULT] [from: ALG]` Recurrence recognition and encounter recognition are the diagonal and off-diagonal cases of one comparator algebra through degree $p-2$; R6's unification sub-item is closed.

`[COR]` **Frame correction (external audit, confirmed by independent $H_2$ conjugation here).** Dyadic §6.9's $c_{32}=-\tfrac{77}{216}+i\tfrac{97\sqrt3}{648}$, $c_{05}=-\tfrac{61}{2160}-i\tfrac{43\sqrt3}{6480}$ are coefficients of the **raw** six-step map in the linear eigenframe, where the quartic jet is nonzero; $47/54=|\mu|^2+2\Re c_{32}^{\rm raw}$ and $\Im\beta^{\rm raw}=c_\theta^{(6)}$ are identities in that frame. Conjugating $T^6$ by $H_2=\mathrm{id}+h_2$ (Dyadic §6.2) gives zero quartic jet, unchanged $\mu$, and
$$c_{32}^{\rm NF}=-\tfrac{97}{432}+i\tfrac{197\sqrt3}{324},\qquad c_{05}^{\rm NF}=-\tfrac{49}{8640}-i\tfrac{307\sqrt3}{25920}=6\bar\lambda\,\nu_5^{(1,6)}\ \text{(Dyadic §6.11, exact)}.$$
A cubic residual gauge cannot generate the charge $-5$ channel, so $c_{05}^{\rm NF}$ is the lattice coefficient of the normal form; $c_{32}$ remains subject to the residual-gauge caveat of Dyadic §6.9. v0.3 used the raw values inside a normal-form model; those numbers are superseded (Appendix B) and restated below in the post-$H_2$ frame where computed; the remaining raw-frame quantities are historical; the chart-native values are those of the completed §6.14 ledger (R11 CLOSED). [cert: `recog_cell_d_lattice_memory_cert.py` rev. 4, checks 1–6 derive both frames from the primitive map.] [cert: `recog_cell_c_lattice_readability_cert.py`, 6 checks — raw-frame identities, retained as identities of their inputs only.] With $W=\sqrt Qe^{i\Theta}$ in the post-$H_2$ frame:
$$\chi_6=1+\mu Q+\bigl(c_{32}^{\rm NF}+c_{05}^{\rm NF}e^{-6i\Theta}\bigr)Q^2+\cdots,\qquad \log\chi_6=\mu Q+\bigl(\beta^{\rm NF}+c_{05}^{\rm NF}e^{-6i\Theta}\bigr)Q^2+\cdots,\quad\beta^{\rm NF}=c_{32}^{\rm NF}-\tfrac{\mu^2}{2}=\tfrac{137}{432}+i\tfrac{89\sqrt3}{324}.$$
(Raw frame: $\beta^{\rm raw}=\tfrac{5}{27}-i\tfrac{119\sqrt3}{648}$ with $\Im\beta^{\rm raw}=c_\theta^{(6)}$ — a raw-frame identity.) Recognition is phase-blind through order $Q$ and acquires sixfold angular modulation at order $Q^2$ in either frame.

`[ALG]` [RID: RECOG-ALG-LATTICE-STRENGTH] $|\mu|^2=19/12$; $\mathcal I_{\rm lat}:=|c_{05}^{\rm NF}|/|\mu|^2\approx0.0134426$ (post-$H_2$; the v0.3 value $0.0192569$ was the raw-frame number), invariant under $W\to cW$ (both scale as $|c|^{-4}$) and under $U(1)$-equivariant residual gauge. Operationally, lattice amplitude / leading comparator amplitude $=\mathcal I_{\rm lat}\,|\mu|Q$. `[historical, raw frame — superseded by the completed §6.14 ledger]` $|c_{05}|/|\beta|\approx0.0828$, $|c_{05}|/|\Re\beta|\approx0.165$, $|c_{05}|/|\Im\beta|\approx0.096$; $\arg c_{05}^{\rm NF}=-105.45^\circ$ vs raw $-157.85^\circ$, so the sixfold axes of v0.3 are also superseded. No amplitude $Q_{\rm lat}$ at which anisotropic and isotropic quintic content cross exists (both $\propto Q^2$); the "leading-crossing" value $|\mu|/|c_{05}^{\rm raw}|\approx41.27$ (raw six-step frame; post-$H_2$ $|\mu|/|a_{05}|\approx59.12$) is outside the local regime in either frame.

`[ALG consequence]` [RID: RECOG-ALG-LATTICE-CLOCK] $\Psi_{\rm lat}:=\arg c_{05}-6\Theta\pmod{2\pi}$ is exactly invariant under linear $W\to cW$ ($\tilde c_{05}=c\bar c^{-5}c_{05}$, $\tilde\Theta=\Theta+\phi$) but not under nonlinear equivariant gauge ($\widetilde W=e^{ia|W|^2}W$ shifts it by $-6aQ$); the asymptotic ratio below is unaffected. Along the backbone $\Theta_m\approx\Theta_0-\tau\log m$, successive thresholds $\Psi_{\rm lat}=\psi_0+2\pi k$ (accumulated defect $\pi/3$ each) occur at $m_k=A(x_0,\psi_0)e^{k\pi/(3\tau)}(1+o(1))$, hence $m_{k+1}/m_k\to e^{\pi/(3\tau)}=\lambda_{\rm DSI}^{1/6}=(R^*)^{1/3}=2.476632\ldots$ [import: RTG_NUMBER_GENESIS_DSN_v1.20.7 | NUMGEN-THM-N5AS1-DEFECT-THRESHOLD-RATIO] [import: Dyadic §6.3 constants]. Per-threshold modulation $A_{{\rm lat},k}\sim|c_{05}|/m_k^2$ fades by $\lambda_{\rm DSI}^{-1/3}$ per threshold; relative to the leading residue by $\lambda_{\rm DSI}^{-1/6}$. The prefactor is orbit- and convention-dependent at subleading order; the ratio is universal in the local $p=6$ healing sector.

`[ALG consequence, leading order]` [RID: RECOG-ALG-LATTICE-MEMORY] [cert: `recog_cell_d_lattice_memory_cert.py` rev. 4, 26 checks] Let $z_m=c_{05}Q_m^2e^{-6i\Theta_m}$. The direct phase kick $\Im z_m$ and the radial kick $2Q_m\Re z_m$ (propagated through $Y=1/Q$: $\delta Y=-2\Re[Z_0(x^{6i\tau}-1)/(6i\tau)]$, fed back via $-\tau Q$) sum absolutely, giving
$$\boxed{\Lambda(Q_0,\Theta_0)=\Delta\Theta_{\rm lat}(\infty)=Q_0\,\Im\!\Bigl[\tfrac{1-2i\tau}{1-6i\tau}c_{05}^{\rm NF}e^{-6i\Theta_0}\Bigr]+O(Q_0^2)=\tfrac{Q_0}{1270080}\bigl[43\sin6\Theta_0-5611\sqrt3\cos6\Theta_0\bigr]+O(Q_0^2)},$$
with $|1-6i\tau|=7$ exactly, $\tfrac{1-2i\tau}{1-6i\tau}=\tfrac{17+4i\tau}{49}$, $\tfrac{1-2i\tau}{1-6i\tau}c_{05}^{\rm NF}=-\tfrac{43+5611\sqrt3\,i}{1270080}$ (raw-frame v0.3 value $-\tfrac{2767+1219\sqrt3\,i}{317520}$ superseded). Invariant form: $\Lambda=\tfrac q\gamma\Im[\tfrac{1-2i\sigma}{1-6i\sigma}c_{05}e^{-6i\Theta_0}]+O(q^2)$, $\gamma=-2\Re\mu$, $\sigma=-\Im\mu/\gamma$ (canonical: $\gamma=1,\sigma=\tau$); $\tfrac q\gamma c_{05}e^{-6i\Theta_0}$ is exactly frame-invariant. Numerics (post-$H_2$ truncated model with/without $c_{05}^{\rm NF}$, rev. 4; unchanged from rev. 3): relative error $0.22,0.11,0.050$ at $Q_0=0.04,0.02,0.01$ — an $O(Q_0)$ correction, as expected. No retained multiplicative/$\log Q$ memory ($|\Delta\log Q|<10^{-6}$ on all runs; a bounded log-periodic oscillation in $1/Q$ is not excluded). Leading amplitude $|\Lambda|\le0.007652\,Q_0$ — leading order only, for the truncated model; not a finite-$q_0$ bound for the full exact memory, whose certified statement is RECOG-THM-R9-MEMORY (§6.13a; SG-R10).

`[ALG consequence]` [historical truncated-model diagnostic; theorem: §6.13a] **Comparator-completeness counterexample.** Pairs $\mathcal P_\alpha$: $(Q_a,Q_b)=(q,q)$, $(\Theta_a,\Theta_b)=(\alpha,\alpha+\tfrac\pi6)$. All $\mathcal P_\alpha$ have identical comparator data ($Q_b/Q_a=1$, $\Delta\theta=\pi/6$), yet retain $\Delta\Phi_{\rm lat}(\alpha)=-2\Lambda(q,\alpha)+O(q^2)$, which depends on $\alpha$ (certified, rev. 4: $+3.13\times10^{-4}$ vs $-3.12\times10^{-4}$ at $q=0.02$, $\alpha=0,0.5$ for identical post-$H_2$ data, and $+3.45\times10^{-4}$ vs $-3.35\times10^{-4}$ for identical canonical raw comparator data; $\alpha=0.25$ lies near a node of the post-$H_2$ lattice phase). `[RESULT] [from: ALG]` $(Q_a/Q_b,\Delta\theta_{ab})$ is insufficient to determine asymptotic memory once $c_{05}\ne0$; the missing datum is the lattice phase. Comparator closure fails at the memory layer, at $O(Q_0)$.

#### §6.13a Retained memory of the exact map (R9) `[THM]` {#sec-6-13a-r9}

> **[SG-R13, SG-R14 apply]** (rep: NF chart $z$ of DYAD-ALG-NF6, track $C_z$) (transport: exact six-step normalised return $\widehat F=h^{-1}F_Wh$).

`[DEF]` **Observable.** $q=|z|^2$; $\Theta$ = continuous lift of $\arg z$ along the orbit (each step $|\widehat F(z)/z-1|<1$); $\Psi(z)=\Theta-\tau\log q$, $\tau=\tfrac{2\sqrt3}{3}$; $M(z_0)=\Psi_\infty-\Psi(z_0)$, $\Psi_\infty=\lim_m\Psi(z_m)$. One orbit, one chart, no comparison dynamics. `[DEF]` **Averaging function.** $g(\Theta)=c_{\rm iso}+\Im(Ge^{-6i\Theta})$, $\Phi=\Psi+q\,g(\Theta)$, with $c_{\rm iso}=\Im[(1-2i\tau)\beta]=-\tfrac{4\sqrt3}{27}$ ($\beta=a_{32}-\mu^2/2$) and $(1-6i\tau)G=(1-2i\tau)a_{05}$, i.e. $G=-\tfrac{43+5611\sqrt3\,i}{1270080}$, $|G|=\tfrac{\sqrt{481897}}{90720}\approx0.0076520$.

`[THM]` [RID: RECOG-THM-R9-MEMORY] [scope: conditional on postulated $T$; exact normalised return $\widehat F$ in the DYAD-ALG-NF6 chart; comparator track $C_z$] For $0<q_0\le q_{\max}$: $M(z_0)=q_0[c_{\rm iso}+\Lambda_1(\Theta_0)]+R$, $|R|\le C_2q_0^2$, with $c_{\rm iso}=-\tfrac{4\sqrt3}{27}$, $\Lambda_1(\Theta)=\tfrac{43\sin6\Theta-5611\sqrt3\cos6\Theta}{1270080}$; rows (not mixed): $(C_2,q_{\max})=(517,\tfrac1{400})$ on $|z|\le\tfrac1{20}$, $(163,\tfrac1{1600})$ on $|z|\le\tfrac1{40}$.

*Proof.* The $q^2$-increment of $\Psi$ is $[c_{\rm iso}+\Im((1-2i\tau)a_{05}e^{-6i\Theta})]q^2$ and $\Delta(qg)=-(g+\tau g')q^2+O(q^3)$; the choice of $g$ makes $g+\tau g'$ equal to that increment (cohomological identity checked exactly), so $|\Delta\Phi|\le K_\Phi q^3$ on the row's domain, with $K_\Phi$ built from the jet coefficients, $C_7$ (DYAD-THM-NF-REM) and four elementary remainder inequalities, all constants enclosed in Arb ($K_\Phi\le258.49$ / $81.44$). Forward invariance and $\sum_mq_m^3\le2q_0^2$ (NF-REM) give convergence of $\Phi(z_m)$; $q_mg\to0$ gives $\Psi_\infty=\Phi(z_0)+\sum_m\Delta\Phi_m$, hence $|R|\le2K_\Phi q_0^2=C_2q_0^2$. $\square$ No Euler–Maclaurin step and no fitted backbone are used.

`[THM]` [RID: RECOG-THM-R9-COMPARATOR-INCOMPLETENESS] [scope: as RECOG-THM-R9-MEMORY, per row; witness window $q_0<|G|/C_2$] **Corollary (exact-map $C_z$ comparator incompleteness).** For $q_a=q_b=q_0$, $\Theta_b-\Theta_a=\delta$: $\lim_m(\Theta_{b,m}-\Theta_{a,m})=\delta+q_0[\Lambda_1(\Theta_a+\delta)-\Lambda_1(\Theta_a)]+(R_b-R_a)$, $|R_b-R_a|\le2C_2q_0^2$ (the $\tau\log(q_b/q_a)$ term vanishes since $1/q_m=m+O(\log m)$). With $\delta=\pi/6$ the sixfold harmonic reverses sign and a suitable $\Theta_a$ gives difference $2|G|$; the lattice memory is therefore certified nonzero whenever $q_0<|G|/C_2$. Depends on: RECOG-THM-R9-MEMORY.

| Row | domain | $q_{\max}$ | $C_2$ | memory theorem | certified incompleteness window |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| large | $|z|\le\tfrac1{20}$, $C_7=100$ | $\tfrac1{400}$ | $517$ | $0<q_0\le\tfrac1{400}$ | $q_0<|G|/517\approx1.4800743\times10^{-5}$ |
| small | $|z|\le\tfrac1{40}$, $C_7=30$ | $\tfrac1{1600}$ | $163$ | $0<q_0\le\tfrac1{1600}$ | $q_0<|G|/163\approx4.6944689\times10^{-5}$ |

The memory theorem holds on the whole row; the incompleteness witness only on the smaller window. The window is $C_7$-limited (a $[{\rm NUM}]$ check on $J$ gives $|M-q_0g|/q_0^2\approx0.934$, flat in $q_0$, so $C_2$ is conservative by $\sim$170–550); the deferred $C_7$ sharpening would widen it roughly proportionally.

**Typed interface and lemmas.**

`[DEF]` [RID: RECOG-DEF-A1A-PHASE-INTERFACE] For $x_0\in U=\{0<Q_0\le\tfrac1{625}\}$ and the orbit $x_m=T^{6m}x_0$, three objects are tied to the same orbit: (i) the **A1a extension** $D_M^U:=2\pi M-(\hat\phi_{6M}-\hat\phi_0)$ for every $x_0\in U$ — NUMGEN's formula, made unambiguous on $U$ by RECOG-THM-A1A-LIFT-ALIGNMENT; on $U\cap U_{\rm NUMGEN}$, $D_M^U=D_M$. This extends a definition, not a NUMGEN theorem, and changes no custody; (ii) Dyadic's block phase $\Theta_W(m)$ from principal per-block increments $\operatorname{Arg}(W_{m+1}/W_m)$ with the lift tie; (iii) $z_m=h^{-1}(W_m)$. $\Psi_W:=\Theta_W-\tau\log Q$, $M_W:=\Psi_{W,\infty}-\Psi_W(0)$ where the limit exists. RECOG owns the typed join, not the three objects.

`[THM]` [RID: RECOG-THM-A1A-LIFT-ALIGNMENT] [scope: $U_T$ of DYAD-THM-PRIM-CTR] [proof: exact algebra + elementary enclosure] For every $x\in U_T$, $\tfrac\pi6<\delta_T(x)<\tfrac\pi2$, where $\delta_T$ is the primitive one-step angle increment. Hence for $x_0\in U$ and every integer $M\ge1$: $D_M^U=\Theta_W(0)-\Theta_W(M)$.
*Proof.* $W(Tx)=\lambda W+\lambda(\eta-\sin\eta)+\omega(e^\rho-1-\rho)$ exactly ($\omega^2=-1-\omega$). On $U_T$, $\lvert\rho\rvert,\lvert\eta\rvert\le\tfrac2{\sqrt3}\lvert W\rvert<2R$, $R=\tfrac{47}{1000}$, and $2R<\ln2$; so $\lvert\eta-\sin\eta\rvert<\tfrac86\lvert W\rvert^3$, $\lvert e^\rho-1-\rho\rvert<4\lvert W\rvert^2$, and $\lvert W(Tx)/(\lambda W)-1\rvert<4R+\tfrac43R^2=\tfrac{143209}{750000}<\tfrac12$. Then $\lvert\operatorname{Arg}(1+\varepsilon)\rvert<\arcsin\tfrac12=\tfrac\pi6$ gives $\delta_T\in(\tfrac\pi6,\tfrac\pi2)$; six-step totals lie in $(\pi,3\pi)$, so each block increment of $\hat\phi$ is $2\pi+\operatorname{Arg}(W_{m+1}/W_m)$, and telescoping gives the identity. $\square$ Check: RECOG-ART-A1A-C1-CHECK.

`[THM]` [RID: RECOG-THM-A1A-RECIPROCAL-AMPLITUDE] [scope: $x_0\in U$] $\lvert Q_{m+1}^{-1}-Q_m^{-1}-1\rvert<\tfrac{15}2\sqrt{Q_m}$; hence $Q_M^{-1}=Q_0^{-1}+M+O(\sqrt M)$ and $MQ_M\to1$.
*Proof.* By DYAD-THM-PRIM-CTR, $Q_{m+1}=Q_m(1-Q_m+\varepsilon_m)$ with $\lvert\varepsilon_m\rvert\le\tfrac{16931}{10000}Q_m^{3/2}+108Q_m^2$, so $Q_{m+1}^{-1}-Q_m^{-1}=1+\theta_m$, $\theta_m=(Q_m-\varepsilon_m/Q_m-\varepsilon_m)/(1-Q_m+\varepsilon_m)$, and with $\sqrt{Q_m}\le\tfrac1{20}$: $\lvert\theta_m\rvert\le\tfrac{7.17}{0.99}\sqrt{Q_m}<\tfrac{15}2\sqrt{Q_m}$. With $Q_m\le\min(Q_0,2/m)$, $\sum_{m<M}\lvert\theta_m\rvert=O(\sqrt M)$. $\square$ (Sufficient, not sharp.)

`[ALG]` [internal lemma; no RID] [scope: $x_0\in U$, $M\ge1$] **Finite-block identity.** $D_M^U-\tau\log M=\tau\log Q_0-\bigl(\Psi_W(M)-\Psi_W(0)\bigr)-\tau\log(MQ_M)$. Depends on: RECOG-THM-A1A-LIFT-ALIGNMENT.

`[THM]` [RID: RECOG-THM-A1A-PHASE-EXISTENCE-U] [scope: conditional on postulated $T$; $x_0\in U=\{0<Q_0\le\tfrac1{625}\}$; block base points $x_m=T^{6m}x_0$, $Q_m=Q(x_m)$, $a:=Q_0^{-1}$; $N$ is the block count] [proof: written below; proof cell `records/recog/RECOG_v0_3_23_FC21_PROOF_CELL.md`] For every $x_0\in U$, H-NG holds, and
$$C_D^{\rm ext}(x_0)=\lim_N\bigl(D_N^U-\tau\log N\bigr)=\tau H(a)+\tau\sum_{m\ge0}\Bigl(Q_m-\frac1{a+m}\Bigr)-\sum_{m\ge0}r_m,\qquad H(a)=-\log a+\sum_{m\ge0}\Bigl[\frac1{a+m}-\log\Bigl(1+\frac1{a+m}\Bigr)\Bigr]=-\psi(a),$$
with all three series absolutely convergent, $\psi$ the digamma function and $r_m$ the row-A block remainders below. Consequently (RECOG-THM-A1A-PHASE-EXISTENCE-ALT) $\Psi_{W,\infty}$ and $\Psi_{z,\infty}$ exist, $\Psi_{z,\infty}=\Psi_{W,\infty}=\Theta_W(0)-C_D^{\rm ext}$ and $C_D^{\rm ext}=\tau\log Q_0-M_W$. RECOG-THM-R9-MEMORY and NUMGEN INF-1 are not used. Depends on: [import: RTG_DYADIC_OSCILLATOR_DSN_v6.4.22 §6.14 (producer edition v6.4.19) | DYAD-THM-LPHASE-ROWA-REMAINDER] (row A; block base points in $U$, intermediate steps in $U_T$; not conditional on R9): $\hat\varphi_{6(m+1)}-\hat\varphi_{6m}=2\pi-\tau Q_m+r_m$, $\lvert r_m\rvert\le\tfrac{403}{250}Q_m^{3/2}$, where $\hat\varphi$ is the increasing lift with principal one-step increments $\delta_T$ and $Q_m$ is the primitive amplitude (Dyadic interface I1–I5); RECOG-THM-A1A-LIFT-ALIGNMENT; RECOG-THM-A1A-RECIPROCAL-AMPLITUDE; DYAD-THM-PRIM-CTR; RECOG-THM-A1A-PHASE-EXISTENCE-ALT.
*Proof.* (1) *Same lift and clock.* On $U_T$, $\tfrac\pi6<\delta_T<\tfrac\pi2$ (RECOG-THM-A1A-LIFT-ALIGNMENT), so the increasing lift of $\arg W$ in $D_N^U$ is Dyadic's $\hat\varphi$, sampled at the same block base points $x_m\in U$ ($F(U)\subset U$). Telescoping, $D_N^U=\sum_{m<N}\bigl[2\pi-(\hat\varphi_{6(m+1)}-\hat\varphi_{6m})\bigr]=\sum_{m<N}(\tau Q_m-r_m)$. (2) *Remainders.* $Q_m\le Q_0/(1+\tfrac12Q_0m)\le\min(Q_0,\tfrac2m)$ (DYAD-THM-PRIM-CTR), so $\sum_m\lvert r_m\rvert\le\tfrac{403}{250}\sum_mQ_m^{3/2}<\infty$. (3) *Amplitudes.* By RECOG-THM-A1A-RECIPROCAL-AMPLITUDE, $Q_m^{-1}=a+m+e_m$ with $\lvert e_m\rvert<\tfrac{15}2\sum_{k<m}\sqrt{Q_k}=O(\sqrt m)$; hence $Q_m-\tfrac1{a+m}=-\tfrac{e_mQ_m}{a+m}=O(m^{-3/2})$. (4) *Harmonic part.* Since $\sum_{m<N}\log(1+\tfrac1{a+m})=\log\tfrac{a+N}a$, $\sum_{m<N}\tfrac1{a+m}-\log N=\sum_{m<N}\bigl[\tfrac1{a+m}-\log(1+\tfrac1{a+m})\bigr]-\log a+\log\tfrac{a+N}N\to H(a)$, the bracket being $O((a+m)^{-2})$; the limit equals $-\psi(a)$. Writing $\tau\sum_{m<N}Q_m-\tau\log N=\tau\sum_{m<N}(Q_m-\tfrac1{a+m})+\tau(\sum_{m<N}\tfrac1{a+m}-\log N)$ and adding (2)–(4) gives the limit (H-NG) and the series. RECOG-THM-A1A-PHASE-EXISTENCE-ALT, whose proof uses neither R9 nor INF-1, gives the rest. $\square$

RECOG-RESULT-A1A-PHASE-EXISTENCE-NG and RECOG-RESULT-A1A-CD-AGREEMENT are unchanged as the NUMGEN-route records; RECOG-THM-A1A-CD-IDENTIFICATION keeps its R9 route. `[NUM]` illustration, not a proof input: RECOG-ART-A1A-EXISTENCE-U (§8).

**Slip schedule on explicit $U$ (A1b, v0.3.24; new science, separately reviewed).**

`[THM]` [RID: RECOG-THM-A1B-SLIP-SCHEDULE-U] [scope: conditional on postulated $T$ (OPEN-P0); $x_0\in U=\{0<Q_0\le\tfrac1{625}\}$; lifted primitive chart, frame $W=\rho+\omega\eta$, increasing lift $\hat\varphi$ with principal one-step increments $\delta_T$; token conventions of §6.16a (F2(b)); block count $M$, $a:=Q_0^{-1}$] [proof: session cell `records/recog/RECOG_A1B_A1C_SESSION_v0_1.md` §2 (Lemmas A, B, C′ and Proposition); certificate `recog_a1b_sixstep_deficit_cert.py`, exact rational gates G6–G8 (RECOG-ART-A1B-SIXSTEP-DEFICIT, §8)] Let $n_0<n_1<\cdots$ be the anchors of the canonical B-events of the orbit ($\eta(x_n)<0\le\eta(x_{n+1})$, $\rho>0$ on both sides; anchor $=n+1$), $g_{j+1}=n_{j+1}-n_j$ the return gaps, and $M_k$ the block index of the $k$-th gap equal to $7$ ($n_{j_k}=6M_k+r_k$, $0\le r_k\le5$). Then for every $x_0\in U$:
(A) *Events are lift crossings.* $x_n\to x_{n+1}$ is a canonical B-event iff $\lfloor\hat\varphi_{n+1}/2\pi\rfloor=\lfloor\hat\varphi_n/2\pi\rfloor+1$.
(C′) *Six-step deficit.* For every $y\in U_T$, with $\Lambda_6(y)$ the six-step lift increment from $y$ and $\Delta(y):=2\pi-\Lambda_6(y)$: $\Delta(y)>0.927\,\tau Q(y)>0$ and $|\Delta(y)-\tau Q(y)|<1.82\,Q(y)^{3/2}$.
(B) *Alphabet.* Every gap is $6$ or $7$; the number $s(M)$ of $7$-gaps among the returns in the first $6M$ steps satisfies $s(M)=\tfrac{3\tau}{\pi}\log M+O(1)\to\infty$.
(S) *Schedule.* With $\psi$ the digamma function and $L(x_0):=\tfrac\pi3n_0-2\pi\ell_0+\Psi_{W,\infty}(x_0)$, $\ell_0=\lfloor\hat\varphi_{n_0}/2\pi\rfloor$:
$$\tau\,\psi(a+M_k)-(k-1)\tfrac\pi3=L+\varepsilon_k,\qquad |\varepsilon_k|\le\tau Q+1.82\,Q^{3/2}+5\arcsin\bigl(0.705\sqrt Q+0.257\,Q\bigr)+\tau\,|S^{\rm tail}_{M_k}|+|R^{\rm tail}_{M_k}|,$$
where $Q$ is the largest primitive amplitude among the points of the $k$-th slip block and $S^{\rm tail}_M=\sum_{m\ge M}(Q_m-\tfrac1{a+m})$, $R^{\rm tail}_M=\sum_{m\ge M}r_m$ are the tails of the series of RECOG-THM-A1A-PHASE-EXISTENCE-U; $|\varepsilon_k|=O(\sqrt{Q_{M_k}})=O((a+M_k)^{-1/2})$. Consequently $\tau\log M_k-(k-1)\tfrac\pi3=L-\tau\log(1+a/M_k)+O(M_k^{-1})+\varepsilon_k\to L$ and $M_{k+1}/M_k\to e^{\pi/(3\tau)}$.
Depends on: RECOG-THM-A1A-PHASE-EXISTENCE-U (existence of $\Psi_{W,\infty}$; the series and their tails; $C_D^{\rm ext}=\Theta_W(0)-\Psi_{W,\infty}$); RECOG-THM-A1A-PHASE-EXISTENCE-ALT; RECOG-THM-A1A-LIFT-ALIGNMENT ($\tfrac\pi6<\delta_T<\tfrac\pi2$ on $U_T$; six-step totals in $(\pi,3\pi)$); RECOG-THM-A1A-RECIPROCAL-AMPLITUDE; [import: RTG_DYADIC_OSCILLATOR_DSN_v6.4.22 §6.14 | DYAD-THM-LPHASE-ROWA-REMAINDER] (branch (B): $|W(Ty)/(\lambda W(y))-1|\le0.04654$ on $U_T$; row-A remainder $|r_m|\le\tfrac{403}{250}Q_m^{3/2}$); [import: RTG_DYADIC_OSCILLATOR_DSN_v6.4.22 §6.14 | DYAD-THM-NF-REM, ingredients] (raw-vector tail $|R^{\rm prim}_{\ge10,\rm vec}|\le332000|W|^{10}$ on $|W|\le\tfrac1{16}$; the degree 7–9 vector ceilings as a cross-check only); [import: DYAD-THM-PRIM-CTR] ($U_T$; $Q_m\le Q_0/(1+\tfrac12Q_0m)$); [import: DYAD-THM-C21] (six-step cubic coefficient $\mu$, reproduced exactly by the certificate); §6.16a F2(b) token conventions and RECOG-DEF-EVENT-TOKEN-SCHEMA. Token predicate and slip-law wording: NUMGEN GAP-LOCK (custody unchanged; explicit-domain instance).
*Proof sketch (full proofs in the cell).* (A): on $U_T$ the sign of $\eta$ and the lift crossing are tied exactly by $\eta'-\eta=e^\rho-1-\sin\eta$ and the branch bound. (C′): the six-step map in $W$ is $W+\mu|W|^2W+\sum_{d=4}^9F_d+R_{\ge10}$ with the $F_d$ computed exactly in $\mathbb Q(i\sqrt3)$; $\Lambda_6=2\pi+\operatorname{Arg}(W(T^6y)/W(y))$ (lift alignment), so $\Delta=-\operatorname{Arg}(1+\mu Q+E)$ with $|E|\le H\,Q^{3/2}$, $H<\tfrac{1783}{1000}$ by rational coefficient ceilings; the elementary arctan bounds give both inequalities uniformly on $U_T$ (certificate gates G6–G8, exact rationals, $\tau$ bracketed). (B): a gap $\ge8$ needs $\Lambda_7(x_{n_j})<2\pi-o_j\le2\pi$, impossible since $\Lambda_7>\tfrac{7\pi}3-7\arcsin0.04654>2\pi$; a gap $\le5$ needs $o_j+\Lambda_5(x_{n_j})\ge2\pi$, impossible since $o_j<\delta_T(x_{n_j-1})$ gives $o_j+\Lambda_5(x_{n_j})<\Lambda_6(x_{n_j-1})<2\pi$ by (C′) at the pre-anchor point; the count law follows from (A) and $D_M=\tau\log M+O(1)$. (S): the exact event identity $G_{n_j}=C+\tfrac\pi3s_j-o_j$, $C=\tfrac\pi3n_0-2\pi\ell_0+\hat\varphi_0$, with $0\le o_{j_k}<\Delta_{j_k}$ at a $7$-gap anchor; the partial-block correction is bounded by the pointwise one-step bound $|\delta_T-\tfrac\pi3|\le\arcsin(0.705\sqrt Q+0.257Q)$; the finite-block identity $D_M=C_D^{\rm ext}+\tau\psi(a+M)-\tau S^{\rm tail}_M+R^{\rm tail}_M$ converts $D_{M_k}$ into the digamma form; $\hat\varphi_0$ cancels between $C$ and $C_D^{\rm ext}$. $\square$

`[ALG]` [RID: RECOG-ALG-A1C-TAIL-HARMONICS] [scope: $x_0\in U$; every $M\ge1$] `[assuming: Dyadic row-A bridge constants $c_Q=0.79246$, $c'_Q=0.36915$ ($|D_Q|\le c_Qq^{3/2}$, $|D_Q-D_3|\le c'_Qq^2$) and the remainder decomposition $r=\Lambda_3+{\rm rest}$ with its constants, as published in the proof of DYAD-THM-LPHASE-ROWA-REMAINDER (certified by DYAD-ART-LPHASE-ROWA); proof constants, not theorem rows; registration requested (§7)]` [proof: session cell `records/recog/RECOG_A1C_TAIL_AVERAGING_SESSION_v0_1.md` v0.2.1 §§1–3 (Props. 1–2, Cor. 3, Lemma 6); certificate RECOG-ART-A1C-SHARPENED-SCHEDULE, part 1b] (i) $|S^{\rm tail}_M|\le\bar S'(a,M)$ and $|R^{\rm tail}_M|\le\bar R'(a,M)$ with explicit closed forms obtained by summing the two angular harmonics of $D_3=2\operatorname{Re}(\bar zh_2)$ ($\phi_1=-\tfrac56-i\tfrac{\sqrt3}6$, $\phi_3=\tfrac1{12}+i\tfrac{\sqrt3}{12}$) and of $\Lambda_3=L_\Theta+\tau D_3$ against the Hurwitz zeta ($\sum_{m\ge M}m^{-3/2-ik\tau}\sim M^{-1/2-ik\tau}/(\tfrac12+ik\tau)$): $\bar S'\sqrt{a+M}\to|\phi_1|/|\tfrac12+i\tau|+|\phi_3|/|\tfrac12+3i\tau|=0.74850$ and $\bar R'\sqrt{a+M}\to1.0753$ (part 1: $\approx150$ and $9.12$; the order $M^{-1/2}$ is not removed); (ii) $Q_M\le\bar Q_M$ with $\bar Q_M\approx1/(a+M)$ (chart reciprocal law $|q_{m+1}^{-1}-q_m^{-1}-1|\le\gamma_0q+\gamma_1q^2$, $\gamma_0=0.17715$, $\gamma_1=205.15$); (iii) the envelope $E'(a,M)=\tau\bar Q^*+1.82\bar Q^{*3/2}+5\arcsin(0.705\sqrt{\bar Q^*}+0.257\bar Q^*)+\tau\bar S'+\bar R'$ majorises the error term of the schedule (S) exactly as the theorem's ceiling does, with $E'\sqrt{a+M}\to5.4646$ (part 1: $187.31$); and `[ALG via IA]` $E'(a,M)\le0.31$ for every $a\ge625$, $M\ge1$ (Lemma 6, one-variable majorant on interval boxes; the proved statement is the ceiling, not the location of the supremum), so every known-$a$ window is informative modulo $2\pi$. Depends on: RECOG-THM-A1B-SLIP-SCHEDULE-U; DYAD-THM-NF-REM, DYAD-THM-NF-INV, DYAD-ALG-NF6 ($h_2$); the listed assumption.

`[ALG]` [RID: RECOG-ALG-A1C-INBLOCK-ANGLE] [scope: $y\in U_T$; primitive frame $W=\sqrt Qe^{i\theta}$; no Dyadic proof constant] [proof: cell `records/recog/RECOG_A1C_INBLOCK_ANGULAR_SESSION_v0_1.md` v0.2 (Lemma 12, Prop. 10); certificate, in-block part] (i) One-step angle law: $\delta_T-\tfrac\pi3=\sqrt Q\,g(\theta)+\varrho$, $g(\theta)=\tfrac23\cos^2(\theta-\tfrac\pi6)\sin(\tfrac\pi3-\theta)$, $|\varrho|\le c_\delta Q$, $c_\delta=0.793$; at the six block phases $\theta=-s\tfrac\pi3$: $g=0$ for $s\in\{1,2,4,5\}$, $g(-\pi)=-\tfrac{\sqrt3}4$, $g(0)=+\tfrac{\sqrt3}4$ — four silent phases, the two others cancelling over a block (the reason the six-step deficit has no $\sqrt Q$ term). (ii) Partial block at a slip anchor $n_{j_k}=6M+r$: $P_r=\sum_{i<r}(\tfrac\pi3-\delta_T(x_{6M+i}))=\kappa(r)\sqrt{Q_\star}+\varrho_P$, $\kappa=(0,0,0,\tfrac{\sqrt3}4,\tfrac{\sqrt3}4,\tfrac{\sqrt3}4)_r$, $|\varrho_P|\le c_P(r)Q^\dagger$ with explicit $c_P(r)$ ($c_P(5)\approx15$), $Q_\star$ the anchor amplitude and $Q^\dagger$ the in-block ceiling; the overshoot $o_{j_k}\in[0,\Delta_{j_k})$ is centred at $\tfrac\tau2(a+M)^{-1}$. Depends on: RECOG-THM-A1A-LIFT-ALIGNMENT; RECOG-THM-A1B-SLIP-SCHEDULE-U (Lemmas A, B, C′); DYAD-THM-PRIM-CTR ($U_T$); DYAD-THM-LPHASE-ROWA-REMAINDER (branch (B)).

`[ALG]` [RID: RECOG-ALG-A1C-COMBINED-TAIL] [scope: $x_0\in U$, chart row A, branch (B)] [proof: cell `records/recog/RECOG_A1C_COMBINED_TAIL_SESSION_v0_1.md` v0.2 (Prop. 13, Cor. 14); certificate, combined-tail part, gate G1 exact] With the owner's decomposition $r_m=S_\Theta(z_m)+\tau D_Q(z_m)+[\operatorname{Arg}(J/z_m)+\tau q_m]+\operatorname{Arg}(\widehat F/J)(z_m)$ and $Q_m=q_m+D_Q(z_m)$, the bridge terms cancel exactly in $\tau(Q_m-\tfrac1{a+m})-r_m$ and the angular increments $S_\Theta(z_m)=D_\Theta(z_{m+1})-D_\Theta(z_m)$ telescope:
$$\tau S^{\rm tail}_M-R^{\rm tail}_M=\tau A_M+D_\Theta(z_M)-\Sigma^J_M-\Sigma^7_M,\qquad A_M=\sum_{m\ge M}\Bigl(q_m-\tfrac1{a+m}\Bigr),\quad|\Sigma^J_M|\le c_J^R\sum_{m\ge M}q_m^2,\quad|\Sigma^7_M|\le c_7^R\sum_{m\ge M}q_m^3,$$
$c_J^R=0.5008$, $c_7^R=100.23$ (RECOG-derived from the explicit jet and $C_7$). The only term of order $M^{-1/2}$ is the single boundary value $D_\Theta(z_M)=\sqrt{q_M}\,d(\Theta_M)+O(q_M)$, $d(\Theta)=-\tfrac{\sqrt3}{12}\cos\Theta+\tfrac14\sin\Theta-\tfrac{\sqrt3}{24}\cos3\Theta-\tfrac1{24}\sin3\Theta$: the separately bounded oscillatory tails (leading constant $\tau\cdot0.7485+1.0753=1.9396$) sum to it (combined harmonic amplitudes $\tfrac1{\sqrt{12}}+\tfrac1{12}=0.3720$, exactly the amplitudes of $d$, because $L_\Theta$ is the spiral derivative of $D_{\Theta,1}$). At a slip block the base angle is $-r\tfrac\pi3+O(\sqrt Q)$ and $d(-r\tfrac\pi3)=\mp\tfrac{\sqrt3}8$ ($-$ for $r\le2$, $+$ for $r\ge3$), so $\kappa(r)-d(-r\tfrac\pi3)=\tfrac{\sqrt3}8$ for every $r$. Depends on: RECOG-ALG-A1C-TAIL-HARMONICS (amplitude brackets), RECOG-ALG-A1C-INBLOCK-ANGLE; DYAD-THM-LPHASE-ROWA-REMAINDER (the decomposition of $r$ under branch (B)); DYAD-THM-NF-REM ($C_7$, $c_1$ row).

`[THM]` [RID: RECOG-THM-A1C-SHARPENED-SCHEDULE] [scope: as RECOG-THM-A1B-SLIP-SCHEDULE-U; $a$ known] `[assuming: Dyadic row-A bridge constants $c_Q=0.79246$, $c'_Q=0.36915$ ($|D_Q|\le c_Qq^{3/2}$, $|D_Q-D_3|\le c'_Qq^2$) and the remainder decomposition $r=\Lambda_3+{\rm rest}$ with its constants, as published in the proof of DYAD-THM-LPHASE-ROWA-REMAINDER (certified by DYAD-ART-LPHASE-ROWA); proof constants, not theorem rows; registration requested (§7)]` [proof: the three blocks above assembled in the combined-tail cell §2 (Cor. 14) and the start-offset cell `records/recog/RECOG_A1C_START_OFFSET_SESSION_v0_1.md` v0.2 §4 (Cor. 19); certificates RECOG-ART-A1C-SHARPENED-SCHEDULE and RECOG-ART-A1C-DECODER] For every $x_0\in U$ and every $7$-gap $k$, with the known-$a$ / declared-data centre $c'''_k(a)$ — a function of the known $a$ and of the record's $(M_k,r_k)$; nothing in this theorem is record-only, the unknown-$a$ record-only operation being RECOG-ALG-A1C-JOINT-CONTRACTOR (§6.16e):
$$c'''_k(a):=\tau\,\psi(a+M_k)-(k-1)\tfrac\pi3+\tfrac{\sqrt3}8(a+M_k)^{-1/2}+\tfrac\tau2(a+M_k)^{-1}=L+\varepsilon'''_k,\qquad|\varepsilon'''_k|\le E'''(a,M_k,r_k),$$
where $E'''$ is an explicit function of the same declared data $(a,M_k,r_k)$ — $E'''=\xi_{\max}+c_P(r)Q^\dagger+\kappa(r)\,{\rm ref}_\kappa+\tau(A_0+A_1)+|\varrho_D|_{\max}+\tfrac{\sqrt3}8\,{\rm ref}_d+c_J^R\Sigma_2+c_7^R\Sigma_3$ with $\tau A_0\le\tau\Delta_a/(a+M)$ the unknown-start-offset term and $\tau A_1$ the chart reciprocal drift — and $E'''(a,M_k,r_k)=O_a\bigl((1+\log(a+M_k))/(a+M_k)\bigr)=o((a+M_k)^{-1/2})$; a pure $O((a+M_k)^{-1})$ is not claimed (the logarithmic term is $\tau A_1$). When the start offset is enclosed (RECOG-ALG-A1C-START-OFFSET, §6.16e), the centre $c^{(4)}_k=c'''_k-\tau\,{\rm mid}(\omega S_{M_k})$ and envelope $E^{(4)}_k=E'''_k-\tau A_0+\tau\,{\rm half}(\omega S_{M_k})$, $S_M=\sum_{m\ge M}\tfrac1{(a+m)(b+m)}$, replace the $\sqrt a$-proportional term by $\approx\tau\cdot0.9/(a+M_k)$, the order unchanged. Depends on: RECOG-THM-A1B-SLIP-SCHEDULE-U (the exact slip identity $\varepsilon_k=-o_{j_k}-P_{r_k}+\tau S^{\rm tail}-R^{\rm tail}$); RECOG-ALG-A1C-TAIL-HARMONICS; RECOG-ALG-A1C-INBLOCK-ANGLE; RECOG-ALG-A1C-COMBINED-TAIL; the listed assumption. The adopted theorem is unchanged; this is an additional statement in the same scope, not a replacement.
`[NUM]` [scope: illustration, not a proof input; RECOG-ART-A1C-SHARPENED-SCHEDULE] At $a=625$ (worst $r$): $E'''=0.029$ at $M=10^3$ ($E''$ of the in-block cell $0.078$; $E'$ $0.166$; part 1's $E$ $2.92$), $4.3\times10^{-3}$ at $10^4$, $4.5\times10^{-4}$ at $10^5$, $4.7\times10^{-5}$ at $10^6$, $5.8\times10^{-6}$ at $10^7$; $E'''(a+M)\to$ about $45$, $52$, $69$ for $a=625,1000,2500$ on the grid. The comparisons $E'''<E''<E'$ hold on the tested grid ($a\in\{625,1000,2500\}$, $M\in\{10^3,\ldots,10^7\}$, all $r$); no universal ordering is claimed.

**Chart reciprocal drift centring (A1c, v0.3.26; new science, separately reviewed).** Notation as above; $H_m(b):=\psi(b+m)-\psi(b)=\sum_{j<m}\tfrac1{b+j}$ and $\tilde b_m:=b+m-\tfrac{29}{216}H_m(b)$.

`[ALG]` [RID: RECOG-ALG-A1C-RECIPROCAL-DRIFT] [scope: $x_0\in U$, chart row A, block base points; adopted rows only — DYAD-ALG-NF6 ($\mu$, $a_{32}$, $a_{05}$), DYAD-THM-NF-REM ($C_7=100$, one-orbit contraction), the part-1b closed forms and $c_\theta$; no Dyadic proof constant] [proof: cell `records/recog/RECOG_A1C_DRIFT_CENTERING_SESSION_v0_1.md` v0.3 §§1–3 (Lemmas 21–22, Prop. 23) and §2a (the proof table of Lemma 22); certificate RECOG-ART-A1C-DRIFT-CENTERING] (i) *Exact first order.* $\delta_m:=q_{m+1}^{-1}-q_m^{-1}-1=-\tfrac{29}{216}q_m-2\operatorname{Re}(a_{05}e^{-6i\Theta_m})q_m+R_m$, $|R_m|\le\gamma_2q_m^2$, $\gamma_2=212.6$ (of which $2C_7=200$ is the NF-REM tail ceiling $|2\operatorname{Re}(\rho_7/z)|/q\le2C_7q^2$): the isotropic coefficient is the exact $1-|\mu|^2-2\operatorname{Re}a_{32}=-\tfrac{29}{216}$ and the sixth harmonic has zero mean. (ii) *Centred drift.* $\sup_{m\ge0}|q_m^{-1}-\tilde b_m|\le K_\epsilon(a)$, an explicit constant without logarithm: $K_\epsilon=\tfrac{29}{216}\Lambda_{\rm iso}+2|a_{05}|\,\Omega+\gamma_2(2q_0+q_0^2)$ with $\Lambda_{\rm iso}\ge\sum_j|q_j-\tfrac1{b+j}|$ (convergent), $\Omega\ge\sup_m|\sum_{j<m}q_je^{-6i\Theta_j}|$ (Euler–Maclaurin for $\sum_j(b+j)^{-1\mp6i\tau}$, $|\zeta(1\pm6i\tau,x)|\le\tfrac1{6\tau}+\tfrac{1+|s|}{2x}$, plus the convergent amplitude- and phase-deviation sums under the phase model $\Theta_j=\Theta_0+\eta_\infty-\tau\log\tfrac{b+j}b+(\eta_j-\eta_\infty)$, $|\eta_j-\eta_\infty|\le\tau\Lambda_{A_1}(j)+\tfrac\tau{b+j}+c_\theta\Sigma_2(j)$; the constant $\eta_\infty$ factors out); $K_\epsilon=0.7203$, $0.4513$, $0.1860$ at $a=625,1000,2500$ ($98$ % from the $C_7$ term; the v0.3.25 majorant $\bar\epsilon_m$ is $2.2$ at $m=10^5$ and unbounded). The bound holds for **every $m\ge0$ and every admissible $b\in[b',a+\Delta_a]$**: each of the three sums is bounded uniformly in $m$ by a row of the proof table (record v0.3 §2a, rows D1–D8: the part-1b bracket, the two closed-form tail majorants with their monotone-regime and small-$j$ cases, the phase model with $|\psi(x)-\log x|\le1/x$, the partial-sum identity $\sum_{j<m}(b+j)^{-s}=\zeta(s,b)-\zeta(s,b+m)$ with the first-order Euler–Maclaurin bound of the Hurwitz zeta at $\operatorname{Re}s=1$, and the one-orbit sum $\sum_jq_j^2\le2q_0+q_0^2$); every row is monotone in $b$ and evaluated at $b'$; the certificate's gate PT1 checks each row's elementary fact and reproduces $K_\epsilon$ from the rows. (iii) *Centring identity.* $A_1=\sum_{m\ge M}(q_m-\tfrac1{b+m})=\Lambda^{\log}_M(b)+A_1'$ with the explicit $\Lambda^{\log}_M(b)=\sum_{m\ge M}\tfrac{29}{216}H_m(b)/((b+m)\tilde b_m)$ (enclosed over an interval of $b$ in this dependency-free form, two-sided closed-form tail) and $|A_1'|\le K_\epsilon P_2'(M)$, $P_2'$ a closed-form majorant of $\sum_{m\ge M}(q_m^{-1}\tilde b_m)^{-1}$ — no logarithm, no constant floor. Depends on: DYAD-ALG-NF6; DYAD-THM-NF-REM; RECOG-ALG-A1C-TAIL-HARMONICS (the reciprocal law $\gamma_0,\gamma_1$, $c_\theta$, the bracket $q_m^{-1}\ge b'+m-\bar\epsilon_m$ and the corrected closed forms).
`[NUM]` [scope: illustration, not a proof input; RECOG-ART-A1C-DRIFT-CENTERING] Jet model ($\rho_7=0$; 12 orbits, $3\times10^5$ blocks): $\sup_m|q_m^{-1}-\tilde b_m|=0.0066$–$0.0113$ ($\le0.061$ of $K_\epsilon$ — the jet cannot exercise the $C_7$ term); without the centre the drift reaches $0.64$–$0.83$ and exceeds $K_\epsilon$ (NC1); with the wrong sign it exceeds $K_\epsilon$ (NC2); the model's $A_1$ lies in $\Lambda^{\log}\pm K_\epsilon P_2'$ (12/12).
**Not supplied:** a bound on the exact-map drift sharper than $K_\epsilon$ (a Dyadic bound on $\operatorname{Re}(\rho_7/z)$ along an orbit, or the B1-L2 ceiling $C_7\le85.1$, would lower it); any event, carrier or Q1′ statement (SG-R6).

`[ALG]` [RID: RECOG-ALG-A1C-LOGFREE-ENVELOPE] [scope: as RECOG-THM-A1C-SHARPENED-SCHEDULE with the start offset enclosed (RECOG-ALG-A1C-START-OFFSET); $a$ known] `[assuming: Dyadic row-A bridge constants $c_Q=0.79246$, $c'_Q=0.36915$ ($|D_Q|\le c_Qq^{3/2}$, $|D_Q-D_3|\le c'_Qq^2$) and the remainder decomposition $r=\Lambda_3+{\rm rest}$ with its constants, as published in the proof of DYAD-THM-LPHASE-ROWA-REMAINDER (certified by DYAD-ART-LPHASE-ROWA); proof constants, not theorem rows; registration requested (§7)]` [proof: cell v0.3 §3 (Cor. 24) and §3a (sum form, covering, small $M$, the all-$M$ constants); certificate RECOG-ART-A1C-DRIFT-CENTERING, gates G3c, G5, PT2–PT4] **Log-free centred envelope.** With $b\in[b^-,b^+]$ from the start-offset iteration: $c^{(5)}_k=c^{(4)}_k-\tau\,{\rm mid}\,\Lambda^{\log}_{M_k}([b^-,b^+])$ and
$$E^{(5)}_k=E^{(4)}_k-\tau\bar A_1-{\rm ref}_d^{\rm old}+\tau\,{\rm half}\,\Lambda^{\log}_{M_k}+\tau K_\epsilon P_2'(M_k)+{\rm ref}_d^{\rm new},$$
${\rm ref}_d^{\rm new}=\tfrac{\sqrt3}8\max(\sqrt{q^+}-n^{-1/2},n^{-1/2}-\sqrt{q^-})$ on the log-free bracket $q_{M}^{-1}\in[\tilde b_M(b^-)-K_\epsilon,\tilde b_M(b^+)+K_\epsilon]$, $n=a+M_k$; $|\varepsilon^{(5)}_k|\le E^{(5)}_k$ by RECOG-ALG-A1C-RECIPROCAL-DRIFT (iii) and the validity of every enclosure. Identically, $E^{(5)}$ is the sum of **ten non-negative terms** $T_1,\ldots,T_{10}$ — $\xi_{\max}$, $\varrho_P$, ${\rm ref}_\kappa$, $|\varrho_D|$, $c_J^R\Sigma_2$, $c_7^R\Sigma_3$, $\tau\,{\rm half}(\omega S)$, $\tau\,{\rm half}\,\Lambda^{\log}$, $\tau K_\epsilon P_2'$, ${\rm ref}_d^{\rm new}$ (the subtracted $\tau\bar A_1$ and ${\rm ref}_d^{\rm old}$ are summands of $E'''$; no `Lg`, no uniform $\kappa$ remains), and **every term satisfies $T_i\le c_i(a,r)/(a+M)$ for every $M\ge1$ with explicit constants** (record v0.3 §3a: the table of the $c_i$ with their derivations from the closed forms and elementary inequalities; $C(a,r)=\sum_ic_i=58.0$, $61.0$, $75.1$ at $r=5$ for $a=625,1000,2500$ with the widest admissible $b$-range of width $2\Delta_a$, the bulk of it the start-offset term; certificate PT3 checks $T_i\,(a+M)\le c_i$ at $28$ values of $M$ from $1$ to $10^{13}$, PT2 the sum form against the difference form) — hence $E^{(5)}(a,M,r)\le C(a,r)/(a+M)$ for all $M\ge1$: no constant floor, no logarithm. The $\Lambda^{\log}$ enclosure uses the monotonicity of $\Lambda^{\log}_M(b)$ in $b$ (endpoints enclosed with the $\psi$ bracket) and an explicit second-order closed-form tail (record §3a; the cell's first enclosure carried an $O(\log n/n)$ slack of relative size $10^{-5}$, found by this check). Depends on: RECOG-ALG-A1C-RECIPROCAL-DRIFT; RECOG-THM-A1C-SHARPENED-SCHEDULE (corrected forms) and its centred form $E^{(4)}$; RECOG-ALG-A1C-START-OFFSET; the listed assumption (through $E^{(4)}$ and $\Delta_a$).
`[NUM]` [scope: illustration, not a proof input; RECOG-ART-A1C-DRIFT-CENTERING] Grid ($r=5$, illustrative $b\in[a-1,a+1]$; an observation of the computed range, not the proof of the bound): $E^{(5)}(a+M)=20.6,19.6,19.3,19.2,19.15,19.15,19.15$ at $a=625$ for $M=10^4,\ldots,10^7,10^9,10^{11},10^{13}$ ($18.53$ at $a=2500$) against $E^{(4)}(a+M)=21.9,21.9,23.7,34.9,1153,1.1\times10^5,1.1\times10^7$ (the floor of the v0.3.25 majorant); the limit value is dominated by the in-block residual $c_P(r)Q^\dagger$ ($14.4$ at $r=5$), $|\varrho_D|$ ($2.3$), the start-offset slack ($1.15$) and $\tau K_\epsilon P_2'$ ($0.83$). On the $73$ slips of the 12 sampled orbits ($1.8\times10^6$ steps; the start-offset $b$-ranges): $|\varepsilon^{(5)}_k|\le E^{(5)}_k+L_{\rm tol}$ at $73/73$ (ratio $\le0.79$ with the reference-$L$ tolerance $L_{\rm tol}\approx10^{-4}$); at the latest slips $E^{(5)}\approx10^{-5}<L_{\rm tol}$, so there the test is a consistency check dominated by the tolerance (ratio $10.7$ without it), a limitation shared with the $E^{(4)}$ test; gains $E^{(4)}/E^{(5)}=1.02$–$1.87$ ($73/73>1$), modest at these $M$ because the in-block and start-offset terms dominate.

### §6.14 The $h$-Chart Ledger (R11) `[ALG / import]` {#sec-6-14-chart-ledger}

> **[Local guards SG-R13, SG-R14 apply]** · **[SG-R7: conditional on the postulated generator $T$]**

`[import: Dyadic v6.4.10 §6.14; current counterpart v6.4.22 §6.14, quoted statements present at the cited RIDs in the current source (claim audit v0.3.23)]` **Owner package (accepted).** Chart $W=h(z,\bar z)$, $h=\mathrm{id}+h_2+\cdots+h_6$, resonant monomial coefficients of each $h_n$ zero (`DYAD-ALG-NF6`; 23 coefficients in $\mathbb Q(i\sqrt3)$, table in the P2-B v2 evidence archive); six-step jet $J(z)=z+\mu z^2\bar z+a_{32}z^3\bar z^2+a_{05}\bar z^5$, $\mu=-\tfrac12-\tfrac{2i\sqrt3}{3}$, $a_{32}=-\tfrac{97}{432}+\tfrac{197i\sqrt3}{324}$, $a_{05}=-\tfrac{49}{8640}-\tfrac{307i\sqrt3}{25920}=6\bar\lambda n_{05}$; obstruction $c_{05}^{\rm raw}-a_{05}=\tfrac{-13+3i\sqrt3}{576}\ne0$ (`DYAD-ALG-NF6-OBS`); inverse chart on $|z|\le\tfrac1{10}$, $|W|\le\tfrac9{100}$, $\tfrac{225}{256}|z|^2\le Q_{\rm prim}\le\tfrac{289}{256}|z|^2$ (`DYAD-THM-NF-INV`); $|\widehat F-J|\le C_7|z|^7$, $\|D\widehat F-DJ\|_2\le C_D|z|^6$ with $(C_7,C_D)=(100,1000)$ on $|z|\le\tfrac1{20}$ and $(30,250)$ on $|z|\le\tfrac1{40}$, rows not to be mixed; $\widehat U_T\subset\{|z|<\tfrac1{20}\}$; $0<q-\tfrac32q^2\le|\widehat F(z)|^2\le q-\tfrac12q^2$ on $0<q\le\tfrac1{400}$, $\sum q_m^3\le2q_0^2$ (`DYAD-THM-NF-REM`); primitive contraction $(108,\tfrac1{400},\tfrac1{625},\tfrac12)$ with tube $U_T$ (`DYAD-THM-PRIM-CTR`). Independent checks in this session: the referee harness §3 (20/20) and a reconstruction of the degree-9 jet, $h$, $J$, $D_{\rm poly}$ (§8). The values $a_{32},a_{05}$ coincide with RECOG's post-$H_2$ $c_{32}^{\rm NF},c_{05}^{\rm NF}$ (v0.3.1): higher $h_3..h_6$ leave the resonant quintic slots unchanged.

`[ALG]` [RID: RECOG-ALG-CHART-LAWS] [cert: `recog_r11_chart_ledger_cert.py` checks 1–4] **Chart-native laws (finite-jet identities from $J$).** With $z=\sqrt qe^{i\Theta}$:
$$|J(z)|^2=q-q^2+\Bigl[\tfrac{245}{216}+2\Re\bigl(a_{05}e^{-6i\Theta}\bigr)\Bigr]q^3+O(q^4),\qquad \tfrac{245}{216}=|\mu|^2+2\Re a_{32};$$
$$\arg\frac{J(z)}{z}=-\tau q+\Bigl[\tfrac{89\sqrt3}{324}+\Im\bigl(a_{05}e^{-6i\Theta}\bigr)\Bigr]q^2+O(q^3),\qquad \tfrac{89\sqrt3}{324}=\Im\bigl(a_{32}-\tfrac{\mu^2}{2}\bigr).$$
With `NF-REM` these become laws for the exact $\widehat F$ with remainders $O(q^4)$ (amplitude: $|q'-|J|^2|\le(2|J|C_7+C_7^2|z|^6)|z|^7$) and $O(q^3)$ (phase: $|(\widehat F-J)/z|\le C_7|z|^6=C_7q^3$, the per-block term of the R9 budget) respectively. The chart quintic phase coefficient is **positive**, opposite in sign to the archival $c_\theta^{(6)}=-\tfrac{119\sqrt3}{648}$; the chart isotropic quintic amplitude coefficient $\tfrac{245}{216}$ differs from the raw angular mean $\tfrac{47}{54}$. These are the concrete reasons no archival quantity is transferred.

### §6.15c R7 — The Twin $Q_c$ Analogue Degenerates `[ALG / RESULT]` {#sec-6-15c-qc-degeneracy}

> **[Local guard SG-R4]** Diagnostic twin $T_s$ only. **Semantics imported from the owner:** $Q_c=(\arcsin\ln2)^2$ is the ISP *pre-crossing sufficient bound* — "if $Q<Q_c$ at pre-crossing, the critical curve is unreachable at the crossing step" (DYAD-ALG-QC-CRITICAL-AMPLITUDE, Dyadic v6.4.22 §6.4; pinned at v6.4.10 §4 before the RID existed; canonical pre-crossing predicate $\rho<0$, $\sin\eta<0$, $\rho-\sin\eta\ge0$). It is local crossing-step protection, not a global no-rank-loss statement. The twin analogue is posed with exactly these semantics.

`[ALG]` [RID: RECOG-ALG-TWIN-CROSSING-RANK-SAFE] Let $x=(\rho,\eta)$, $x'=T_sx$, $\rho'=\rho-\sin\eta$. (a) Negative-to-nonnegative crossing $\rho<0\le\rho'$: then $-\sin\eta=\rho'-\rho>0$, so $\sin\eta<0$ (the owner's predicate is automatic), and $0\le\rho'<-\sin\eta\le1$. (b) Antipodal crossing $\rho>0\ge\rho'$: $\rho'=\rho-\sin\eta>-\sin\eta\ge-1$, so $-1<\rho'\le0$. Hence every radial sign-crossing step satisfies $|\rho'|<1$. Since $C_0^{(s)}=\{\cos\eta=-1/(\cosh\rho-1),\ |\rho|\ge\operatorname{arccosh}2\}$ and $1<\operatorname{arccosh}2$, **no crossing step lands on $C_0^{(s)}$, for any $Q$.** Quantitatively, $\cosh\rho'-1<\cosh1-1<1$ gives
$$D_s(T_sx)=1+(\cosh\rho'-1)\cos\eta'>2-\cosh1=0.45691936\ldots>0$$
at every crossing landing.

`[RESULT]` `[from: ALG]` [RID: RECOG-RESULT-TWIN-QC-DEGENERACY] Under the crossing-step semantics that define the primitive $Q_c$, the symmetric twin has **no finite amplitude threshold to determine**: crossing-step rank safety is unconditional. No finite $Q_c^{(s)}$ is required; this is not the derivation of an amplitude invariant "$Q_c^{(s)}=\infty$". *Why the systems differ:* the primitive critical branch begins at $\rho=\ln2<1$, inside the maximal radial displacement of a crossing landing, so an amplitude restriction is meaningful; the twin branches begin at $|\rho|=\operatorname{arccosh}2>1$, beyond it. The primitive $Q_c$ is a consequence of the one-sided exponential rank geometry, not a feature every shear variant inherits. Other conceivable "thresholds" are different objects and do not answer the inherited question: an expansion-domain radius (E2A already gives the sufficient phase-uniform domain $0<Q\le1/25$) or the minimum $Q$ on $C_0^{(s)}$ (a static distance to the critical locus). Crossing-step safety is compatible with §6.15b: $C_0^{(s)}$ is reached at non-crossing steps.

`[NUM]` (session sanity check, not an artifact) Four million random states: about 320k crossing steps, maximum $|\rho'|=0.99979$, minimum landing $D_s=0.933\ge2-\cosh1$.
