Not universal over possible state enlargements. | `[RESULT]` / `[DEF consequence]` | **REFUTED within the registered-carrier inventory** |

`[ALG]` **Representation-safety ledger.**

* **Rank event.** The critical locus $\operatorname{rank}DF_x<2$, equivalently $\det DF_x=0$ in the present two-dimensional chart, is invariant under local diffeomorphism: under $y=\varphi(x)$,
$$\det D(\varphi F\varphi^{-1})_{\varphi(x)}=\frac{\det D\varphi_{F(x)}}{\det D\varphi_x}\det DF_x,$$
and the prefactor is nonzero. The zero set is therefore representation-safe.
* **Retained exact-map memory.** RECOG-THM-R9-MEMORY is certified in the registered $C_z$ track. Finite-initial-state comparator data are track-typed (SG-R14), but the chart correction to phase vanishes as $z\to0$; consequently the limiting pair phase is track-independent. The retained observable is asymptotically representation-safe even though its finite-time comparator input is not track-free.
* **RECOG lattice thresholds.** The individual lattice phase $\Psi_{\rm lat}=\arg c_{05}-6\Theta$ is invariant under linear frame changes but shifts under the nonlinear equivariant gauge $\widetilde W=e^{ia|W|^2}W$ by $-6aQ$ (§6.13). Individual threshold locations/indices are therefore representation-convention quantities; the asymptotic clock ratio is invariant. A lattice crossing cannot be promoted to a physical token without an additional representation/tokenisation bridge.
* **Number Genesis physical 7-gap.** The token is defined inside the Number Genesis representation as a primitive return-time event. **v0.3.9:** criterion 1 PASS under the transported section — RECOG-ALG-BTOKEN-TRANSPORT (§6.16b); a native or literal replacement section is a different token rule, not a representation change.

`[DEF candidate]` [RID: RECOG-DEF-EVENT-TOKEN-SCHEMA] **Event-tokenisation schema.** A candidate event rule is a typed map from an admissible relational history to a discrete token stream
$$\mathcal E_{\mathcal R}:\mathcal H_{\mathcal R}\longrightarrow\{(n_j,\sigma_j)\}_{j\ge1},$$
where $\mathcal R$ names the registered representation. This is a schema for testing candidate rules, not an adopted physical definition. A candidate tokenisation is assessed against:

1. **Representation safety.** Event occurrence is invariant/covariant under the registered representation changes, or an explicit bridge transports the token.
2. **Nongenericity.** Generic smooth variation of comparator values does not emit tokens.
3. **Finite witnessability.** Event occurrence is decidable from finite registered state/history data.
4. **Dynamical distinction.** The token marks a change in a registered structural status — e.g. rank/recoverability class, return word, stabiliser, admissible continuation, or another explicitly defined dynamical class — rather than an arbitrary numerical threshold alone.
5. **Consequence type.** The post-token consequence is recorded, without prejudging its physical interpretation, as `R` — retained/recoverable discriminator; `E` — certified loss of recoverability/noninjectivity; `N` — neither established.

Criterion 5 is deliberately a classifier rather than an admissibility axiom. Whether a physical **recognition** event requires `R`, permits `E`, or obeys a stronger no-erasure condition remains outside CELL-F1 (SG-R1: the ontology note owns that reading).

`[RESULT]` `[from: THM]` [scope: registry-relative] **F1 conclusion.** The present package supplies comparison, structural transitions, mathematical threshold streams, and theorem-grade retained memory, but no theorem selects one of these as *the* physical recognition-event predicate. R6 therefore remains **ACTIVE**. The missing object is a representation-safe tokenisation/selection principle, to be tested rather than stipulated.

**CELL-F2 targets** (as posed; all executed: F2(b) §6.16a, F2(a) §6.16b, F2(c0) §6.16c, F2(c1) §6.16d; the refinement F2(d) §6.16e).

* **F2(b): eventwise clock/token test.** On common realised orbits, compare Number Genesis physical 7-gap tokens against lifted RECOG phase-lag level crossings; output an overshoot/residual sequence, not another clock ratio. The Number Genesis definitions of $G$, $M_j$, $s_j$, the section and its phase convention are imported literally from the owner text before the script is written. First grade `[NUM]`; no identification is inferred from equal asymptotic ratios alone.
* **F2(c): rank-transition consequence.** F2(c0) first certifies or refutes the local Whitney-fold condition at the B3 encounter $x_*$ (corank one, kernel field $k$, $d(\det DT)_{x_*}(k)\ne0$). Only if the fold condition is certified, classify the resulting local loss of inverse uniqueness under criterion 5 (`E`) and separately ask whether any registered history variable retains the branch information (`R`). No ontological erasure claim is made in RECOG.

Order used: F2(b) before F2(c0).

`[ALG]` [RID: RECOG-ALG-BTOKEN-TRANSPORT] [scope: registered local diffeomorphic representation changes on their common domain, with the canonical B-section and its predicate transported] Let $\Sigma_B^W=\{W=t,\ t>0\}$ be the Number Genesis canonical section in the raw representation, with its crossing coorientation and the $\rho>0$ side condition included in the owner predicate $E_B^W(W_n,W_{n+1})$. Along corresponding orbits $W_n=h(z_n)$, $E_B^z(z_n,z_{n+1})\iff E_B^W(W_n,W_{n+1})$; hence, exactly, $n_j^z=n_j^W$ and $g_j^z=g_j^W$: the $\{6,7\}$ return word and the 7-gap token survive the representation change. #### §6.16c CELL-F2(c0) — Whitney Fold at the B3 Rank Encounter `[ALG]` {#sec-6-16c-fold}

`[ALG]` [RID: RECOG-ALG-P2-C0-WHITNEY-FOLD] [depends: RECOG-THM-P2-C0-FIRST] Let
$$D(\rho,\eta):=\det DT=1+(e^\rho-1)\cos\eta,\qquad k(\rho,\eta):=(\cos\eta,\,1).$$
Then $DT\,k=(0,D)^T$ identically. Thus on the rank locus $C_0=\{D=0\}$, $k$ spans $\ker DT$; since the first row of $DT$ contains the entry $1$, every point of $C_0$ has $\operatorname{rank}DT=1$. The left-null covector $\ell=(-e^\rho,1)$ satisfies $\ell\,DT=(0,D)$, and $\ell\,D^2T[k,k]=dD(k)$ holds identically.

The fold scalar is
$$\kappa:=dD(k)=e^\rho\cos^2\eta-(e^\rho-1)\sin\eta .$$
Writing $A=e^\rho-1$, the equation $D=0$ gives $\cos\eta=-1/A$, hence on $C_0$
$$\kappa=\frac{A+1}{A^2}-A\sin\eta .$$
The certified box $B_*=\bigl(\tfrac{101667}{50000},\tfrac{101671}{50000}\bigr)\times\bigl(\tfrac{5003}{625},\tfrac{80059}{10000}\bigr)$ of RECOG-THM-P2-C0-FIRST gives $\rho>2$, so $e^\rho>1+\rho+\tfrac{\rho^2}2>5$ and $A>4$; and, using only $3<\pi<\tfrac{22}7$, $\tfrac{5\pi}2<\eta<\tfrac{17\pi}6$, so $\sin\eta>\tfrac12$. Since $(A+1)/A^2$ is strictly decreasing for $A>0$,
$$\kappa<\frac5{16}-4\cdot\frac12=-\frac{27}{16}<0$$
at every point of $C_0\cap B_*$, in particular at the certified first encounter $x_*$. By the corank-one criterion for smooth maps $\mathbb R^2\to\mathbb R^2$ (critical set a smooth curve since $dD\ne0$; kernel transverse to it since $dD(k)\ne0$), $x_*$ — and the whole critical arc through $B_*$ — is a **Whitney fold**. The historical high-precision point gives $\kappa\approx-6.39$, consistent with the bound. [cert: `recog_cell_f2c0_fold_cert.py`, exact SymPy, 7/7; the Taylor bound $e^\rho>5$ and the two $\pi$ gates were re-checked in exact rational arithmetic in the session record]

`[RESULT]` `[from: ALG]` The rank transition at $x_*$ has a certified local fold structure, locally equivalent to $(u,v)\mapsto(u,v^2)$: regular values on the fold side have two local preimages, so the primitive one-step map loses local inverse uniqueness across the critical value. Under RECOG-DEF-EVENT-TOKEN-SCHEMA this supplies consequence type **`E`** at the primitive-map level. It does **not** establish ontological erasure, and it does not decide whether an enlarged registered history state retains the branch discriminator; that `R` question was posed as F2(c1) and is answered in §6.16d:

> **F2(c1).** Does any registered retained-history object preserve the fold-branch discriminator?

#### §6.16d CELL-F2(c1) — Registered Retention at the Fold `[RESULT / DEF consequence / ALG]` {#sec-6-16d-fold-retention}

> **Scope guard.** This cell audits the carriers currently registered in RECOG and its imported local token construction. It does **not** assert that no enlarged state, cocycle, external record, or other unregistered history variable could retain the pre-fold branch. Full replay of the mathematical trajectory is not itself a registered retained carrier.

`[DEF]` **Fold-branch discriminator.** In the lifted primitive chart, let $V$ be a sufficiently small Whitney-fold neighbourhood supplied by RECOG-ALG-P2-C0-WHITNEY-FOLD. At the critical value $y_*=T(x_*)$ itself the fold has the single critical preimage $x_*$; the two-sheet phenomenon is for nearby regular values on the folded side. Choose
$$x_+\ne x_-\in V,\qquad T(x_+)=T(x_-)=y .$$
A registered carrier supplies consequence type `R` for this fold only if it is part of the registered post-event state/history interface and can distinguish the two histories after they have reached the common image $y$. Merely adjoining $x_{n-1}$, a branch bit, a trajectory label, or an external event log would be a new history carrier and is not counted as an existing RECOG object.

`[DEF consequence / ALG]` **Registered-carrier inventory.**

| registered object | defined/carried at the fold? | separates the two branches after the common image? | disposition |
|---|---|---|---|
| primitive lifted state $(\rho,\eta)$, including the lift of $\eta$ | yes | no: $T(x_+)=T(x_-)=y$ gives the same full lifted image state ($V$ lies on one lift sheet) | no `R` |
| present-state pole/state comparator data | yes when their stated domains hold | no: as functions of the common present state they factor through $y$; comparison with $x_\pm$ would require retaining the previous state explicitly (§6.12: a past comparison partner is not automatically in the present state) | no `R` |
| R9 observable $\Psi$ and retained memory $M$ | not on the B3 fold neighbourhood; theorem domain is the local NF sector near $P_1$ | if the common future later enters that sector, both histories start that calculation from the same state and yield the same R9 datum | no `R` |
| Number Genesis B/7-gap counters and token stream | certified on the declared local tube, not as a primitive state component transported through the B3 fold | a pre-fold counter or token record carried through the fold would itself be an additional retained-history register; none is registered as part of $T$'s state | no registered `R` |
| Track-A step/reachability count $n$ | admissible as history/order bookkeeping, but not a component of the instantaneous autonomous state | no: two local histories can reach the common image $y$ at the same count; using $n$ together with a replayed trajectory or prior-event log would add information not carried by the present primitive state | no `R` |
| B3 unstable-manifold parameter, tangent transport, certificate bookkeeping | proof/provenance objects only | not registered observables or transported state variables | inadmissible as `R` witness |

`[RESULT]` `[from: ALG]` [scope: registry-relative; current registered carrier inventory] [RID: RECOG-RESULT-P2-FOLD-NO-REGISTERED-RETENTION] **No registered fold-branch retention.** Within the current registered carrier inventory, the Whitney-fold rank transition has consequence type `E` but no registered companion `R`: nearby distinct local preimages can have the same lifted primitive image, and none of the registered retained-history objects both applies across that fold and preserves a discriminator of which preimage branch occurred.

This is a statement about the **current package**, not a no-memory theorem for all possible enlargements. An augmented carrier containing a previous-state register, branch cocycle, or other dynamically transported history variable could change the classification and would require its own definition and covariance/recoverability audit. Consequently \"rank loss erases information\" remains forbidden as an ontological or model-independent statement.

`[RESULT]` `[from: ALG]` [scope: registry-relative] **Criterion-5 classification.** For the certified B3 rank transition:
$$\text{primitive-map consequence}=E,\qquad\text{registered retained discriminator }R=\text{none}.$$
F2(c1) is CLOSED. This classification does not select the rank transition as the physical recognition event; OPEN R6 remains ACTIVE.

`[RESULT]` `[from: ALG]` [scope: registry-relative] **Separation of the R and E axes — transition and token side.** It also contains transition/noninjectivity without registered retention (§6.16c–d) and a representation-safe, dynamically distinguished token (the B/7-gap, §6.16a–b). #### §6.16e CELL-F2(d) — Retention Classification of the B/7-gap Token `[ALG / RESULT]` {#sec-6-16e-btoken-retention}

> **Scope guard.** This cell concerns the certified Number Genesis local primitive-dyad tube and the owner definitions imported in §6.16a. It distinguishes history-relative bookkeeping from an independent retained carrier. The emitted token log, the counter $s_j$ itself, or full trajectory replay may not be used as the independent carrier witnessing `R`; otherwise criterion 5 would be tautological. No ontological memory claim is made. F2(d) is a criterion-5 refinement prompted by the completed F2(a)–(c) table, not part of the original F2 targets.

`[DEF]` **Event-time defect mark.** With $n_j$ the canonical B-event anchors, $o_j$ their overshoots and $G_n=\tfrac\pi3n-(\widehat\phi_n-\widehat\phi_0)$ (§6.16a), set $H_j:=G_{n_j}+o_j$. Its definition does not use $s_j$ or the labels $g_{j+1}\in\{6,7\}$.

`[ALG]` [RID: RECOG-RESULT-BTOKEN-LOG-EQUIVALENCE] **Exact identity and its collapse.** GAP-LOCK Lemma 3 gives $G_{n_j}=C+\tfrac\pi3s_j-o_j$, so $H_j=C+\tfrac\pi3s_j$ and $H_{j+1}-H_j\in\{0,\tfrac\pi3\}$ according as $g_{j+1}=6$ or $7$. But at B anchors $\widehat\phi_{n_j}=2\pi\ell_j+o_j$ with $\ell_j-\ell_{j_0}=j-j_0$ (one revolution per return), hence
$$H_j-H_{j_0}=\tfrac\pi3(n_j-n_{j_0})-(\widehat\phi_{n_j}-\widehat\phi_{n_{j_0}})+(o_j-o_{j_0})=\tfrac\pi3\big[(n_j-n_{j_0})-6(j-j_0)\big],$$
and with $n_j=n_0+6j+s_j$,
$$s_j-s_{j_0}=(n_j-n_{j_0})-6(j-j_0).$$
The apparent retained slip mark is exactly Track-A elapsed-count bookkeeping plus the number of canonical B returns. The lift winding at B anchors is itself the B-event count — F2(b) rev. 2 rebuilt the owner lift as $\widehat\phi_n=\theta_n+2\pi c_n$ with $c_n$ the literal B-event count, mathematically identically — so neither $H$ nor $\widehat\phi$ supplies a discriminator independent of the token history. That the phase *could* be unwrapped without calling the detector does not change this in the present package: the certified local theory proves its winding increments are exactly the canonical B laps, and RECOG has not registered the winding integer as an independently transported carrier.

`[DEF]` **Criterion-5 refinement.** `R` is split into $R_{\log}$ — formally recoverable from token-equivalent history bookkeeping (token counts, Track-A order), and **inadmissible** as evidence that an event left a non-tautological retained discriminator — and $R_{\rm ind}$ — retention by a registered carrier whose value is not a function of the token history and Track-A order alone. The original schema is unchanged; this is a refinement of its criterion-5 classifier.

`[RESULT]` `[from: ALG]` [scope: registry-relative; anti-tautology guard] Under the anti-tautology guard, the B/7-gap token has $R_{\log}$ only; **$R_{\rm ind}$ is not established**. The theorem-grade owner law $D_{M_k}=C+\tfrac{(k-1)\pi}3+o(1)$ for the block-sampled defect $D_M=2\pi M-(\widehat\phi_{6M}-\widehat\phi_0)$ is dynamically substantive, but it establishes **predictability/locking** of the slip stream from an accumulated phase defect, not a persistent mark written by the token. Guard: this does not say the lift or the defect is mathematically trivial; it says only that the registered package does not supply a distinct carrier whose retained value separates histories beyond what the token count and Track-A order already encode.

#### §6.16f CELL-F3 — Comparator-Quotient Token/Memory Coupling Audit `[DEF / ALG / RESULT]` {#sec-6-16f-coupling}

> **Scope guard.** Certified order and registered single-dyad carriers only. No claim about enlarged states, other substrates, or future event channels. No numerical run: on a comparator fiber both token descriptors and R9 memory are deterministic functions of one hidden phase, so a correlation study would mostly measure two encodings of that phase.

`[DEF]` **F3(a) — Well-posedness.** For $q$ in the R9 incompleteness window and fixed $\delta$, the comparator fiber
$$\mathcal F_{q,\delta}=\bigl\{(z_a,z_b)=(\sqrt q\,e^{i\alpha},\ \sqrt q\,e^{i(\alpha+\delta)})\bigr\},\qquad C_z(a,b)=q\,e^{i\delta}\ \text{fixed},$$
is parameterised by the hidden common phase $\alpha$. The retained pair-phase residue is $\Delta M_{q,\delta}(\alpha)=M(z_b)-M(z_a)$; a token descriptor $\mathcal T_H(\alpha)$ (first-slip residue, finite slip word, slip count over a declared horizon) is evaluated on the same primitive histories. Conditioning on the full state is excluded: $\Psi_\infty(z)=\Psi(z)+M(z)$ is a state function, so any "past-token effect" given the state is Markov-trivial. The coupling question is posed after conditioning on the registered comparator quotient.

`[DEF]` **Coupling ladder.** **C0** — quotient-conditioned covariation, $\mathcal T_H(\alpha)\leftrightarrow\Delta M(\alpha)$ with $C_z$ fixed; **C1** — dependence surviving removal of the registered common drivers; **C2** — token-specific write-through (below). Only C2 deserves the phrase "the token writes into independent memory"; token–memory covariation does not imply write-through.

`[ALG]` **F3(b) — Common driver.** By RECOG-THM-R9-MEMORY,
$$\Delta M_{q,\delta}(\alpha)=q\bigl[\Lambda_1(\alpha+\delta)-\Lambda_1(\alpha)\bigr]+O(q^2),\qquad \Lambda_1(\alpha)=\tfrac{43\sin6\alpha-5611\sqrt3\cos6\alpha}{1270080},$$
(for the certified witness $\delta=\pi/6$: $\Delta M=-2q\Lambda_1(\alpha)+O(q^2)$). The leading residue depends on $\alpha$ only through $6\alpha$: after quotienting the known sixfold dependence, the theorem supplies no remaining variation through $O(q)$. Hence **C1 has no certified signal through $O(q)$**; any residual lies inside the $O(q^2)$ exact-map budget, whose harmonic content R9 does not resolve (the delivered map need not have all-orders $\mathbb Z_6$ symmetry; DYAD-ALG-NF6-DEG10, registered in RRF v0.8 as finding VF-1). Structurally: the registered normal form is $\mathbb Z_6$-equivariant at the certified order ($n(\lambda z)=\lambda n(z)$, resonance $a-b\equiv1\pmod6$), so the leading memory sees only the sixfold phase, while the B-section is a single ray that breaks $\mathbb Z_6$ — token descriptors can carry sector/residue information that the leading R9 memory cannot distinguish. Any observed token/memory relation at this order is C0 through the common driver $\alpha$.

`[DEF]` **F3(c) — C2 token-specific write-through.** Let $E$ be the transition set selected by a registered token predicate and $K$ a registered carrier with update $U_K(x)=K(Fx)-K(x)$ (or the $p$-step version). C2 holds only if the package supplies a registered decomposition $U_K=U_{\rm bg}+U_E$ such that: (1) $U_E$ is specified independently of observing $U_K$ and multiplying by $\mathbf 1_E$; (2) its definition depends explicitly on event occurrence or a separately registered event channel; (3) it transports correctly under the registered representation changes; (4) suppressing that channel with the declared background fixed changes the retained carrier; (5) $U_E$ is not algebraically equivalent to the token log, an event counter, or the Track-A clock. A jump $U_K^+-U_K^-\ne0$ across $\partial E$, or a lower-order derivative discontinuity, is a strong **intrinsic witness** of C2, since it cannot be absorbed into one smooth background law; it is not logically necessary when the theory already supplies an independent event channel (e.g. $K'=K+B(x)+\sigma\Gamma(x)$ with a registered event mode $\sigma\in\{0,1\}$).

`[ALG]` [RID: RECOG-ALG-CONTINUOUS-CARRIER-NOWRITE] [scope: registered single-dyad carriers and registered event predicates] Let $K$ be a registered carrier whose update is smooth on a connected declared domain, and $E$ a token predicate obtained by partitioning transitions of the same dynamics. In the absence of an independently registered event-gated update channel, a decomposition of $U_K$ into "background" and a smooth term attributed to $E$ is non-unique (take $U_{\rm bg}=U_K$, $U_E=0$); hence smooth covariation with $E$ does not establish C2. A threshold crossing of $K$ itself yields at most token/log equivalence $R_{\log}$ (the lift and its winding: carrier trajectory → threshold token, not token → carrier change; §6.16e). An identifiable C2 witness requires either a registered event-gated channel or a non-smooth boundary signature that cannot be absorbed into the smooth background law.

`[RESULT]` `[from: ALG]` [scope: registry-relative] [RID: RECOG-RESULT-R9-NO-TOKEN-WRITETHROUGH] R9's registered independent memory carrier ($\Psi$, $\Phi$) has analytic block updates on the punctured certified domain — $\Phi$ is constructed by a smooth cohomological cancellation — and no registered event-gated channel. Therefore **no identifiable token-specific write-through exists within the presently registered R9 mechanism**: no C2 coupling from the B/7-gap token into R9 memory is established; at the certified order any token/memory covariation is explainable by the common sixfold driver. No independent C0 correlation claim is made. This is scoped to the registered mechanism; it is not a claim that no future carrier could be event-written.

`[RESULT]` `[from: ALG]` [scope: registry-relative] **Ladder outcome and R6 endpoint.**

| level | content | status |
|---|---|---|
| C0 | quotient-conditioned covariation $\mathcal T_H(\alpha)\leftrightarrow\Delta M(\alpha)$ | expected; explained by the common driver $\alpha$ |
| C1 | residual dependence after removing registered common drivers | no certified signal through $O(q)$ |
| C2 | independently identifiable token-gated write-through | absent from the registered single-dyad carriers |

| structure | profile |
|---|---|
| B/7-gap | token + transition + $R_{\log}$ |
| fold | token/transition + `E`, no registered `R` |
| R9 | $R_{\rm ind}$, no token |
| lattice threshold | smooth/readable threshold, not a representation-safe token |

The registered single-dyad package contains **no mechanism** for token → $R_{\rm ind}$ write-through. A recognition event combining token, transition and independent retention requires either (1) a new single-dyad carrier with a genuinely event-gated, non-counting channel, or (2) carrier enlargement — multi-dyad / network / history structure where retention lives outside the autonomous one-dyad state. Route (2) is a routing `[HYP]` only: RECOG blocks continuous↔discrete transfer without a bridge, and the single-dyad $z$-chart does not transport linear network closure (SG-R5, §6.14 network guard); it is not yet a TDR-G consequence. R6 stays ACTIVE.

#### §6.16g CELL-F4 — Identification and Inheritance Ledger (R6 successor) `[DEF / RESULT, registry-relative]` {#sec-6-16g-inheritance}

> **Scope guard.** Identification and inheritance only: no dynamics, no computation, no coding, no continuous↔discrete or single-dyad→network bridge (SG-R5). Every "first undefined arrow" cites an owner statement; nothing is imported by being listed. RRF symbols are used as registered: the bridge is RRF §3's row `T-net`, the arrow `RRF-DYAD-W → RRF-NET-W-RAW`, `[OPEN] [scope: bridge]`; it is cited by its arrow so that the open row is not read as a constructed map.

**Inheritance gates (above the ledger).** RRF §6 dependency lock: **OPEN-B** (genesis identification primitive: node identity vs pole-role identity) → **OPEN-E1** (canonical network state variables; cannot close before OPEN-B) → **OPEN-E2** (joint constrained normal form; starts only after E1; "the single-dyad $h$ is not the answer to E2"). In `RRF-NET-W-RAW`, $W_e^{\rm net}$ "is **not** identified with the isolated-dyad $W$"; and **multiplicative edges are not registered** ($R_e\in\mathbb C^*$ and $H_\times$ need a future registered multiplicative network realization) (RRF §2.5).

**Ledger questions.** Q1 what exactly is the object, and what counts as the same object? Q2 which registered carrier owns it? Q3 under which registered composition/transport does it survive (or N/A)? Q4 which registered inheritance map takes it to a larger carrier, and where does that map first become undefined? Metadata: RID / grade / scope.

**A. Dyad-source inventory.**

| # | package | status | first undefined arrow |

| # | object | what survives | what is not supplied |
|---|---|---|---|
| N1 | provisional $W_e^{\rm net}$ in `RRF-NET-W-RAW`: oriented raw edge relations plus declared incidence/attachment; native operators $D$, $C$ | $CW^{\rm net}=0$ only in the exact/node-mediated branch; the general raw profile admits $CW^{\rm net}\ne0$ (additive holonomy) | identification with dyad $W$ (OPEN-B/E1); multiplicative edges |
| N2 | edge cochain $a$ on a finite directed multigraph; $h=Ca$; $[a]\in H^1(G;F)$, $a\sim a+D^T\phi$ | NG-7A (`[RESULT] [from: ALG]`, field coefficients): $CD^T=0$, $\operatorname{im}D^T=\ker C$, orientation/gauge/cycle-basis invariance — $h$ and $[a]$ are equivalent complete additive obstructions at the operator layer | the physical source of $a$: **NG-7B OPEN** (positive-source candidates: continuous frame/connection data, birth/history data, spin/quaternionic structure, OPEN-M content) |

**Composition-side guard.** Number Genesis M2-1 (transported chain identity, `[RESULT] [from: ALG]`, algebra only): an algebraic chain identity can survive transport without supplying $\Pi$, WD-DOM, carrier closure, or any admitted chain (M2-1 no-closure guard). In this ledger, "survives composition" never means "is carried by a closed physical composition".

`[RESULT]` `[from: DEF]` [scope: registry-relative] [RID: RECOG-RESULT-F4-NETWORK-WELLPOSEDNESS] **Registry-relative endpoint.** The present package does not make a token $\to\Delta[a]$ test well-posed. The B/7-gap token is defined on the certified single-dyad history (S5–S6), while $[a]$ is defined only once a network edge cochain and cycle operator are supplied (N2); no registered inheritance map identifies the token-bearing dyad carrier with an edge/subgraph carrier for $a$. For such a test to become well-posed — without any dynamics being asserted here — the network interface must at minimum supply: **(W1)** a fixed directed graph, coefficient module and edge cochain $a$ with a registered realization; **(W2)** a registered dyad → edge/subgraph attachment (an OPEN-B resolution) under which the chosen token predicate is preserved; **(W3)** event-indexed cochains $a^-$, $a^+$ in the **same** cohomology space, so that $\Delta[a]=[a^+]-[a^-]$ is defined; **(W4)** an independence condition preventing $\Delta[a]$ from being reconstructible from the token log, event count or Track-A clock (otherwise the outcome is $R_{\log}$, as in F2(d)). To pose the R6 C2 question itself, additionally **(W5)** a declared conditioning/common-driver quotient (the network analogue of F3's comparator fiber), so that token-specific write-through is distinguishable from C0 covariation.

`[RESULT]` `[from: DEF]` [scope: registry-relative; no import] **Cross-owner alignment (no import).** (i) A token $\to\Delta[a]$ mechanism satisfying W1–W4 would instantiate one NG-7B "birth/history data" positive-source candidate. Thus the R6 network successor and NG-7B **intersect at this candidate**; neither target is closed by the other (NG-7B owns other source candidates and the broader non-Abelian/source obligations). (ii) CELL-G1's composition premise P1 composes ratios $r_{AC}=r_{AB}r_{BC}$ multiplicatively — a structure RRF has not registered (multiplicative edges, $H_\times$); P1 cannot be posed as a network statement before a registered multiplicative realization exists.

## §8. Artifact and Script Registry {#sec-8-artifacts}

*Repository locations.* Scripts: `evidence/recog/scripts/`. Outputs and certificates: `evidence/recog/outputs/`, and `evidence/recog/<version>/` for small per-release outputs. Frozen packages, kept as received: `evidence/recog/packages/`. Records, notices and reviews: `records/recog/` (A1a in `records/recog/a1a/`, G3 in `records/recog/g3/`). Every file named in this section is in the repository, loose or as a member of a package in `evidence/recog/packages/`, except the Dyadic referee harness `dyad_referee_p2b_v2.py` (Dyadic-side). Items cited here by hash only that are not in the repository: superseded revisions (F2(b) rev. 1, CELL-D rev. 2 and rev. 3), the NUMGEN C4 reply and its receiving review, the A1a cell-draft v0.1–v0.3 reviews and the A1a session v0.3 review; elsewhere in this file, the v0.3.21 receiving review, the Dyadic v6.4.17 release-verification report and the frozen v0.3.5 bytes `3ba74002…abd6`. No certificate on which a current RECOG theorem rests is among them. Per-hash state: `records/recog/RECOG_SOURCE_RECOVERY_LIST.md` and `evidence/recog/inventory/recog_recovery_inventory_v0_3_22.json`.

Scientific corrections and retired claims: Appendix B.

### v0.3.26 — DRAFT, stage 1 (corrected intermediate; not adopted)
- **Basis:** PI decision of 28 Oct 2026 (option (b) for the correction finding of the drift-centering cell, PR #93, review #5674661826; round 2 after the stage-1 review #5675460082 — DRIFT-1, ENV-1: `PREPARE RECOG ASYMPTOTIC CORRECTION + DRIFT-CENTERING SUCCESSOR` — one maintenance round; no long orbit rerun; the Appendix B rationale, the corrected closed forms, the corrected order sentences, and Lemma 21–22, Prop. 23 and the conditional Cor. 24 as separate acceptances). Each group below carries its own acceptance decision.
- **New science, group DRIFT (separately reviewed):** RECOG-ALG-A1C-RECIPROCAL-DRIFT (§6.13b) — exact first order of the chart reciprocal law ($-\tfrac{29}{216}$ isotropic, zero-mean sixth harmonic, remainder $\gamma_2q^2$ with $\gamma_2=212.6$ dominated by $C_7$); the log-free drift constant $K_\epsilon(a)$ ($0.72$–$0.19$) bounding $|q_m^{-1}-\tilde b_m|$ for all $m\ge0$ and every admissible $b$ (proof table, record v0.3 §2a; DRIFT-1 of review #5675460082); the exact centring identity $A_1=\Lambda^{\log}_M(b)+A_1'$; adopted rows only, no (D5); RECOG-ART-A1C-DRIFT-CENTERING (§8); §7 NF6 and NF-REM rows record the uses.
- **New science, group ENV (separately reviewed; requires DRIFT):** RECOG-ALG-A1C-LOGFREE-ENVELOPE `[assuming: (D5)]` (§6.13b) — the centred envelope $E^{(5)}$ as a sum of ten non-negative terms, each $\le c_i(a,r)/(a+M)$ for every $M\ge1$ with explicit constants (record v0.3 §3a; ENV-1 of review #5675460082), no constant floor ($E^{(5)}(a+M)\to19.15$ at $a=625$ on the grid to $10^{13}$, an observation; the bound is term by term); `[NUM]` 73-slip containment with the tolerance caveat; gains $1.02$–$1.87$ at the sampled slips.
- **Unchanged:** every existing RID's statement and grade (the two `[COR]` sentences correct order claims for stated majorants; the inequalities stand); every OPEN status word (R1, R3 PARTIAL; R4, R5, R6 ACTIVE; R2, R7–R11 CLOSED).
- **Records:** release record `records/recog/RECOG_v0_3_26_RELEASE_RECORD.md` (candidate); predecessor tag `recog-v0.3.25` (SHA-256 `86cae8ff…d7db`).

### v0.3.24 — 24 October 2026 — Adopted

## Appendix B — Retired and Rejected Claims

**[SUPERSEDED | RID: RECOG-DEF-SPECTRAL-RECOG]** "Spectral Recognition Hypothesis": recognition $=$ eigenvalue/eigenphase of the return transport. Replaced by: §6.2 (eigenvalue is the linearised recognition multiplier; content is the first nontrivial return jet). Version: v0.1.

**[SUPERSEDED | RID: RECOG-DEF-CLOSURE-PREREQ]** "Without return the spectrum cannot be compared with itself" as a prerequisite of all recognition. Replaced by: §6.5 prerequisite ladder (closure needed for the spectral stratum only). Version: v0.1.

**[SUPERSEDED | RID: RECOG-ALG-QUADRATURE-GLOBAL]** "$\cos\eta=0$ is the unique type-change surface on the whole plane." Replaced by: §6.5 (second branch $\cos\eta=4e^\rho$ for $\rho\le-\ln4$; chart-dependent in any case). Version: v0.1.

**[SUPERSEDED | RID: RECOG-DEF-BREAK-CAUSES-HEALING]** "Healing is caused by swap-symmetry breaking." Replaced by: §6.7 radial-fate law ($4a^2>3b$; $\tanh$ heals without breaking). Version: v0.1.

**[RETRACTED | RID: RECOG-DEF-COMPARATOR-SIGN-V01]** $\overline W_aW_b=I_{ab}+i\tfrac{\sqrt3}{2}\omega_{ab}$. Reason: sign error against the certified Genesis $J_Q$ convention; direct algebra gives $-i$. Replaced by: §6.1, SG-R9. Version: v0.2.

**[RETRACTED | RID: RECOG-ALG-IM-FLOOR-V01]** "$\Im\mu_6\le-\tau/2$ across the shear family." Reason: contradicted by $\Im\mu_6^{(s)}=-\tau/4$. Replaced by: RECOG-ALG-CUBIC-RETURN-RIGIDITY (§6.7). Version: v0.2.

**[SUPERSEDED | RID: RECOG-DEF-RETURN-DEFECT-V01]** Return defect defined over an exact closed history $\gamma$. Replaced by: resonant return germ definition, §6.2. Version: v0.2.

**[SUPERSEDED | RID: RECOG-DEF-COPRESENCE-V01]** "By the history-collapse criterion a past state is not part of the present state" (universal reading of the wrapped-$S^1$ negative control). Replaced by: §6.5 scoped statement (retained relational data or current co-realisation). Version: v0.2.

**[SUPERSEDED | RID: RECOG-NUM-P2-C0-FIRST-BASIS]** RECOG-NUM-P2-C0-FIRST `[NUM strong]` as the basis of the positive-branch $C_0$ claim, and the v0.3.3–v0.3.4 "two futures, mixed grade" statement. Replaced by: RECOG-THM-P2-C0-FIRST `[THM]` (§6.11). The high-precision point $(2.033364438914269\ldots,\,8.005165051501685\ldots)$, the transversality diagnostic $\approx-9.38$ and the lifted itinerary remain as historical evidence. Version: v0.3.5.

**[SUPERSEDED | RID: RECOG-NUM-P2-HET-NEG-CONJ]** "Heteroclinic connection $W^u_-(P_2)\to P_1$ conjectured" (v0.3–v0.3.2, `[NUM strong]`). Replaced by: RECOG-THM-P2-HET `[THM]` (§6.11). The sampling evidence (12-step entry into $Q<0.3$, $Q(P_u(-1))=5.215$, long-orbit decay) is retained as history. Version: v0.3.3.

**[SUPERSEDED | RID: RECOG-ALG-R8-STAGE1-LINEAR-CONE]** Linear-cone local lemma at $P_2$ (parallel working session, not registered). Reason: the quadratic cusp $|w|\le\kappa s^2$ is sharper and is the operative Stage 1 of the registered certificate. Version: v0.3.3.

**[SUPERSEDED | RID: RECOG-DEF-BACKBONE-V03]** The v0.3 "corrected backbone for R9": $Q_m=\tfrac qx-\tfrac{7}{54}q^2\tfrac{\log x}{x^2}$, $\Theta_m=\Theta_0-\tau\log x+qH(x)$ with $H$ built from $\tfrac{47}{54}$ and $c_\theta^{(6)}$, $H(\infty)=-\tfrac{31\sqrt3}{72}$, $A(x)$. Reason: $\tfrac{47}{54}$ is the raw fixed-radius angular mean, not a pointwise law, and $c_\theta^{(6)}$ is an archival Lie-gauge quantity; Dyadic v6.4.10 SG-19 forbids their transfer into the conjugacy chart, and the chart values differ ($\tfrac{245}{216}$; phase coefficient of opposite sign). Replaced by: §6.14 chart laws; R9. Version: v0.3.2.

**[SUPERSEDED | RID: RECOG-DEF-R10-TEMPLATE]** R10 as an unposed template "in the R11-resolved chart". Replaced by: DYAD-THM-NF-REM / DYAD-THM-PRIM-CTR accepted (§7, §6.14). Version: v0.3.2.

**[SUPERSEDED | RID: RECOG-ALG-LATTICE-RAWFRAME-V03]** All v0.3 lattice numbers computed with the raw six-step $c_{32},c_{05}$ as normal-form coefficients: $\mathcal I_{\rm lat}=0.0192569$, $|c_{05}|/|\beta|=0.0828$, axes at $33.69^\circ\pmod{60^\circ}$, transfer coefficient $-\tfrac{2767+1219\sqrt3 i}{317520}$, $|\Lambda|\le0.010962\,Q_0$. Reason: those coefficients belong to the raw frame with nonzero quartic jet; post-$H_2$ values differ and the lattice one is fixed by the Dyadic one-step ledger. Replaced by: §6.13 (post-$H_2$), OPEN R11. Version: v0.3.1.

**[SUPERSEDED | RID: RECOG-DEF-FIX-TWO-POINTS]** "$\operatorname{Fix}(T)=\{P_1,P_2\}$ on the wrapped phase; $\operatorname{Fix}(S_o)=\operatorname{Fix}(T)$." Reason: wrapped cylinder has winding fixed points $(\log(1+2\pi k),0/\pi)$; the pole stratum contains all $\rho$. Replaced by: §6.3, §6.4. Version: v0.3.1.

**[SUPERSEDED | RID: RECOG-DEF-ONLY-RANK-CHARTFREE]** "Only rank is pointwise chart-free." Reason: sign of $\det DF_x$ is also invariant under connected-chart conjugacy. Replaced by: SG-R3. Version: v0.3.1.

**[SUPERSEDED | RID: RECOG-DEF-PSILAT-GAUGE]** "$\Psi_{\rm lat}$ invariant under $U(1)$-equivariant residual gauge." Reason: $\widetilde W=e^{ia|W|^2}W$ shifts it by $-6aQ$. Replaced by: SG-R10, §6.13. Version: v0.3.1.

**[SUPERSEDED | RID: RECOG-DEF-RAW-REMAINDER-O5]** "$T^6(W)=W+\mu_6|W|^2W+O(|W|^5)$ in the linear eigenframe." Reason: raw quartic jet is nonzero. Replaced by: §6.2. Version: v0.3.1.

**[SUPERSEDED | RID: RECOG-NUM-P2-C0-FIRST-V1]** "The first rank-loss encounter lies $8.66^\circ$ inside quadrature, toward the antipode; $81.34^\circ$ is an oriented antipode-to-rank-loss scale." Reason: the unstable branch traverses $278.66^\circ$ of lifted pole angle, crossing $-i,+1,+i$, and reaches $C_0$ $8.66^\circ$ past the second quadrature from the identity side; $81.34^\circ$ is the wrapped shadow and the offset is derived from $\rho_*$. Replaced by: §6.11. Version: v0.3.

**[SUPERSEDED | RID: RECOG-DEF-KRET-OPEN-V021]** "$k_{\rm ret}(p)$ unknown for $p\ne6$; $\mu_p=0$ possible at exceptional horizons." Replaced by: §6.10 (no cubic-silent horizon for $p\ge5$). Version: v0.3.

**[SUPERSEDED | RID: RECOG-DEF-LATTICE-ONSET-EVENT]** "A recognition event is the onset of lattice-harmonic content." Reason: onset is an order in the germ, not a locus; no $Q$-threshold exists. Replaced by: §6.13 clock and retained-memory formulation; R6 memory-version `[HYP]`. Version: v0.3.

**[SUPERSEDED | RID: RECOG-DEF-NEGATIVE-BRANCH-CONFINED]** "$W^u_-(P_2)$ stays in $\rho<0$, hence rank loss is excluded on it." Reason: the branch reaches $\rho\approx0.62$; the lemma covers only segments confined to $\rho<0$. Replaced by: §6.11. Version: v0.3.

**[SUPERSEDED | RID: RECOG-OPEN-SATURATING-SHEAR]** OPEN R5 framed as "why saturating shear". Reason: $e^\rho-1$ is unbounded for $\rho\to+\infty$; the cubic jet cannot test saturation. Replaced by: RECOG-OPEN-ONESIDED-SHEAR. Version: v0.2.

**[FAIL | RID: RECOG-FAIL-K-FROM-SADDLE]** Derive the antipodal band width $k$ from the $P_2$ saddle spectrum alone. Reason: eigenvalues fix width–lifetime scaling, not width; invariant under local rescaling. Closed: v0.1.
